<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作物生长预测对农业生产系统的生产力、韧性和运营管理至关重要，但现有方法在国家级大尺度、多年份场景下的LAI轨迹预测能力尚不清楚，且云遮挡和重访间隔导致LAI监督信号稀疏，模型生成的轨迹在有效观测之间振荡不切实际。

Method: 将作物生长预测建模为超越最后一个可用Sentinel-2观测的未来LAI轨迹预测任务；提出轻量级单峰形状正则化器（unimodal shape regulariser）约束轨迹的物理合理性；对比深度学习的序列到序列（Seq2Seq）模型与经典机器学习基线，在覆盖瑞士全境、含2000多万像素级样本的多年份数据集上进行评估。

Result: Seq2Seq模型在跨年份泛化上表现优异，R²超过0.8，持续优于传统方法；引入的单峰形状正则化器在几乎不损失预测精度的前提下显著提升了轨迹的合理性，消除了观测点间的不合理振荡。

Conclusion: 研究证明遥感卫星数据与气象驱动相结合的序列建模能够在景观尺度（国家尺度）上有效学习作物生长动态，为大规模农业监测与管理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Forecasting crop growth across agricultural landscapes is important for improving the productivity, resilience, and operational management of farming systems. In this work, we investigate whether Earth observation time series and meteorological drivers can be used to predict future canopy development at country scale. We focus on winter wheat and formulate crop growth prediction as forecasting future leaf area index (LAI) trajectories beyond the last available Sentinel-2 observation. We evaluate this task on a multi-year dataset which spans the entire country of Switzerland, containing over 20 million pixel-level Sentinel-2-derived LAI time series paired with meteorological variables. Because cloud cover and revisit gaps leave LAI supervision sparse, models fit the few valid (cloud-free) LAI observations yet oscillate implausibly between them, producing trajectories no real canopy could follow. We introduce a lightweight unimodal shape regulariser which improves trajectory plausibility with negligible loss in accuracy. We compare deep learning sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) models with classic machine learning baselines and show that Seq2Seq models generalise well across years, achieving $\mathrm{R}^2$ above 0.8 and consistently outperforming conventional approaches. Together, these results demonstrate that remote sensing and weather-driven sequence modelling can learn crop growth dynamics at landscape scale. S

</details>


### [51] [MAGneT-3D: Monocular and Domain-Generalizable Temporal 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14282)
*Mohamed Kotb,Johannes Meier,Christoph Reich,Oussema Dhaouadi,Luis Denninger,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGneT-3D是首个用于域泛化单目时序3D目标检测的方法，通过领域鲁棒锚点生成器（DRAG）和时序细化与身份合并（TRIM）策略，解决了查询式3D检测器因可学习查询过拟合训练数据分布而导致的跨数据集泛化问题，在零样本跨域场景下将NDS从12.1%提升至18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询式3D检测器的可学习查询会过拟合训练数据的空间分布（如视场角等），这一问题在单目视频场景下尤为严重，严重阻碍了模型泛化到未见过的数据集和环境。现有方法缺乏对单目时序3D检测进行领域泛化的处理手段。

Method: 提出MAGneT-3D框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）Domain-Robust Anchor Generator（DRAG）——在推理过程中自适应地推导3D proposals，替代静态可学习查询；（2）Temporal Refinement and Identity Merging（TRIM）策略——通过时序细化与身份合并来降低对特定3D proposals的依赖，从而增强域泛化能力。同时建立了跨数据集基准（nuScenes、Waymo、Lyft、ONCE）。

Result: 在零样本域迁移场景下，MAGneT-3D在所有基线上均取得最优性能，将NDS从12.1%提升到18.6%，同时在内域（in-domain）精度上也有提升。

Conclusion: 通过DRAG和TRIM策略，MAGneT-3D有效解决了查询式检测器在单目时序3D检测中的域泛化瓶颈，在跨数据集零样本迁移中显著优于现有方法，为单目时序3D检测的域泛化研究提供了新的方向和基准。

Abstract: Monocular temporal 3D detection aims to detect objects in 3D, given a monocular video. Query-based 3D detectors unify detection and cross-view association, but their learnable queries fit the spatial distribution of the training data (e.g., field-of-view). We show that this issue is especially severe when these models are applied to monocular video, hindering generalization to unseen datasets and environments. To address this limitation, we introduce MAGneT-3D, the first method for domain-generalized monocular temporal 3D object detection. Instead of relying on static learnable queries, we propose a Domain-Robust Anchor Generator (DRAG) approach that adaptively derives 3D proposals during inference. To further enable domain generalization, we propose a Temporal Refinement and Identity Merging (TRIM) strategy, reducing dependence on specific 3D proposals. To enable comprehensive domain-generalization evaluation, we establish a cross-dataset benchmark spanning nuScenes, Waymo, Lyft, and ONCE. Under zero-shot domain shifts, MAGneT-3D outperforms all baselines, improving NDS from 12.1% to 18.6% while also increasing in-domain accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Seeing Red, Thinking Bad: Color Bias in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14286)
*Kohsuke Ide,Ryousuke Yamada,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yutaka Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLMs）如何处理以图像形式渲染的文字，并发现视觉样式偏见（如颜色和对比度）会显著影响VLMs的语义理解。作者引入了"隐形视觉提示"（Stealth Visual Prompts），通过微妙改变文字的颜色和对比度等视觉样式而不改变语义内容，系统性地揭示了VLM对文字的颜色偏见和对比度依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs越来越多地应用于工业决策系统（如招聘支持和推荐系统），理解它们如何处理视觉和文本信息至关重要。然而，VLMs如何处理渲染为图像的文字以及视觉样式偏见对其影响尚不清楚，这可能导致VLM在关键决策场景中产生与人类语义理解不一致的错误。

Method: 作者提出了"隐形视觉提示"（Stealth Visual Prompts），通过微妙改变文字的视觉样式（如颜色、对比度）同时保留语义内容。他们利用这些提示系统性地控制文本中词语的视觉样式，测量其对VLM分析的影响，并进一步分析视觉扰动如何影响视觉编码器的潜在表征。

Result: 实验发现：将积极词汇着色为绿色会持续将情感预测推向积极方向，导致VLMs经常无法正确考虑文本中的消极词汇。该行为与颜色变化引起的视觉编码器潜在表征改变相关。此外，降低文字与背景的对比度会增加对视觉显著线索的依赖，导致更多错误的VQA输出。

Conclusion: 渲染文本的视觉样式可以引导VLMs的解释方式，使其偏离人类语义理解。这表明VLMs在视觉-语言融合过程中存在系统性偏见，对依赖VLM的工业决策系统具有重要的安全与公平性启示。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in industrial decision-making systems, such as recruitment support and recommendation. This motivates careful analysis of how VLMs process visual and textual information. In this work, we study how VLMs interpret text rendered as an image, and investigate the influence of visual styling biases. To this end, we introduce Stealth Visual Prompts, which subtly change visual styling of text, such as color and contrast, while preserving semantic content. Using these prompts, we systematically control the visual styling of words in text and measure their impact on the analysis performed by VLMs. We further analyze how such visual perturbations affect the latent representations of the vision encoder. From our experiments, we observed that coloring positive words in green consistently shifts sentiment predictions toward a positive direction. As a result, VLMs often fail to properly account for negative words present in the text. Our analysis suggests that this behavior is correlated with changes in the latent representations of the vision encoder induced by color variations. In addition, we show that reducing text--background contrast increases reliance on visually salient cues and leads to more incorrect Visual Question Answering (VQA) outputs. These results suggest that the visual styling of rendered text can guide VLMs' interpretation in ways that diverge from human semantic understanding.
  Project page: https://github.com/KohsukeIde/color-bias-vlm

</details>


### [53] [Conditional Neural Optimal Transport for Predicting Cellular Phenotypes from Molecular Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14293)
*Gauthier Avité,Maxime Sanchez-Renauld,Nicolas Bourriez,Auguste Genovesio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分子条件的神经最优传输（NOT）模型，从分子结构和阴性对照表型预测化合物诱导的细胞表型，在未见分子上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高内涵显微镜技术可系统刻画细胞对化学扰动的反应，但化学空间规模巨大，穷举表型表征在实验上不可行，因此需要计算模型在无需获取实际处理细胞的情况下预测图像衍生表型。

Method: 将分子诱导表型预测形式化为图像表示空间中的归纳条件传输问题。首先评估经典最优传输基线（静态耦合方法），然后提出分子条件的神经最优传输（NOT）模型，采用Monge-Gap正则化训练目标，以分子结构为条件信息学习将阴性对照未扰动表型传输到扰动表型。在压缩表示空间中执行传输以提升性能和可扩展性。

Result: 静态耦合在大型表型图像数据集上无法产生有效预测；NOT模型在未见活性分子上优于基线方法，能恢复分子特异性表型效应并减少显微镜相关的技术变异，促进跨实验批次比较。分子编码器是泛化的主要瓶颈，压缩表示空间中的传输可改善性能和可扩展性。

Conclusion: NOT框架是从分子结构和阴性对照表型预测细胞表型的有前景方法，未来开发更具信息量的分子表示是提升分布外性能的关键方向。

Abstract: High-content microscopy enables systematic profiling of cellular responses to chemical perturbations, but the scale of the chemical space makes exhaustive phenotypic characterization experimentally infeasible. This motivates computational models that can predict image-derived phenotypes without acquiring the corresponding treated cells. We formulate molecule-induced phenotype prediction as an inductive conditional transport problem in image representation space. Given a negative-control phenotype and the structure of a molecule, we aim to predict the phenotype induced by the corresponding molecule. We first evaluate classical optimal transport baselines and show that static couplings do not yield useful predictions on large-scale phenotypic image datasets. We then introduce a molecule-conditioned Neural Optimal Transport (NOT) model with a Monge-Gap regularization training objective that learns to transport negative-control unperturbed phenotypes toward perturbed phenotypes using molecular structure as conditioning information. NOT recovers molecule-specific phenotypic effects while reducing microscopy-associated technical variation, thereby facilitating comparisons across experimental batches. On unseen active molecules, the model outperforms baseline approaches, demonstrating that chemically conditioned transport can generalize beyond the molecules observed during training. We identified the molecular encoder as the main limitation to this generalization, while transport in a compressed representation space improves performance and scalability. These results establish NOT as a promising framework for predicting cellular phenotypes from molecular structure and negative-control phenotypes, while highlighting the development of more informative molecular representations as a key direction for improving out-of-distribution performance.

</details>


### [54] [Spatial Message Passing in Language Space for Pathology Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14309)
*Jing-Cheng Yang,Hao-Jung Wang,Jinhao Du,Yang Hu,Ming-shan Tsai,Jens Rittscher,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLMP框架通过在纯语言空间中构建空间文本图（瓦片为节点、空间邻接为边），利用LLM的消息传递与可检查的自适应聚合策略，在不微调MLLM权重的情况下实现全切片图像（WSI）的空间推理，显著提升肿瘤描述准确性并弥合通用MLLM与病理专用模型之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM能够从组织学图像生成病理描述，但千兆像素的WSI超出其视觉上下文限制。标准的瓦片切分方法虽然使WSI可处理，却破坏了定义肿瘤-间质界面和形态学的组织邻域结构，丧失空间上下文信息。

Method: SLMP将WSI区域表示为空间文本图：瓦片作为节点并用MLLM描述初始化，边编码空间邻接关系。对每个瓦片，LLM在共享聚合策略下整合相邻瓦片的语言消息来精炼其描述；该策略在瓦片网格上充当作用于文本的自适应局部核，可通过文本梯度从模型观察到的组织表型中自动进行语义优化，且全程无需微调MLLM权重。

Result: 在HER2和CAMELYON16区域上，SLMP在通用和病理专用骨干模型上均提升瓦片级肿瘤描述准确率，增益达+3.3至+19.6个百分点。随机邻居消融实验证实增益来自空间上下文而非额外文本本身；检查优化后的策略可发现可解释的、组织特异性的决策规则。

Conclusion: SLMP无需任何权重更新或骨干MLLM微调，即显著提升通用MLLM性能并缩小其与病理专用模型的差距，为基于MLLM的病理分析提供了一种透明且灵活的空间推理机制。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can generate pathological descriptions from histological images, but gigapixel Whole Slide Images (WSIs) exceed their visual context limits. The standard tiling workaround makes WSIs tractable yet severs the tissue neighborhoods that define tumor-stroma interfaces and morphology. We introduce Spatial Language Message Passing (SLMP), a framework that performs spatial reasoning entirely in language space, human-readable by construction. SLMP represents a WSI region as a spatial text graph: tiles are nodes initialized with MLLM descriptions, and edges encode spatial adjacency. For each tile, an LLM refines its description by integrating language messages from adjacent tiles under a shared aggregation policy that, on the tile grid, acts as an adaptive local kernel operating on text rather than learned embeddings. This policy is an inspectable prompt that can be refined from model-observed tissue phenotypes via textual gradients, enabling automatic semantic optimization from local cellular context to broader tissue morphology without fine-tuning MLLM weights. On representative HER2 and CAMELYON16 regions, SLMP improves tile-level tumor description accuracy in settings spanning general-purpose and pathology-specialized backbones, with gains of +3.3 to +19.6 percentage points. Random-neighbor ablations confirm that these gains stem from spatial context rather than additional text alone, and inspecting the optimized policies reveals interpretable, tissue-specific decision rules. Besides, without any weight updates or fine-tuning the backbone MLLM, SLMP substantially improves general-purpose MLLMs and narrows its gap to pathology-specialized counterparts, offering a transparent and flexible mechanism for incorporating spatial reasoning into MLLM-based pathology analysis.

</details>


### [55] [Intelligent Detection of Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) Metrics Based on 2D Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14317)
*Tarandeep Singh Mandhiratta,ANK Zaman,Abdul-Rahman Mawlood-Yunis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个基于Mask RCNN的神经网络模型，用于从2D平面图中自动检测照明符号、识别灯具类型并提取相关文本，实现了较高的检测精度（bbox_mAP 0.7596，segm_mAP 0.7111），旨在提升建筑设计效率和功耗估算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的平面图设计中，照明设备的识别和功耗估算依赖人工操作，效率低下且容易出错。研究旨在通过自动化提取MEP（机械、电气、管道）对象，提高建筑设计效率，并为节能建筑设计和功耗估算提供支持。

Method: 以Mask RCNN作为基础模型，对2D平面图图像进行标注，转换为COCO数据格式用于训练。模型用于检测照明符号、识别灯具类型，并提取与灯具相关的文本信息。

Result: 模型在边界框检测上达到bbox_mAP 0.7596，在实例分割上达到segm_mAP 0.7111。在不同IoU阈值下表现良好，bbox_mAP 50和segm_mAP 75分别达到0.9850和0.9219。

Conclusion: 该模型能够有效自动检测平面图中的MEP对象，帮助建筑和施工行业缩短设计时间、优化工作流程，是迈向节能建筑设计和功耗估算工具的第一步。

Abstract: This research developed a neural network-based model to extract various information from 2D floor plans. We detect lighting symbols, identify the appropriate type of light, and extract the associated texts with lights. The study aims to enable efficient floor designing and determining the number and type of lights needed per floor, i.e., allow efficient design and estimate the power requirement of the floor plan. The model was developed using Mask RCNN as the base. The images were annotated and converted into a Coco data format for training the model. The model achieved bbox\_mAP and segm\_mAP values of 0.7596 and 0.7111, respectively. It also performed well at different IoU thresholds, i.e., with bbox\_mAP 50 and segm\_mAP 75 values of 0.9850 and 0.9219, respectively. The developed model will help various industries, such as architecture and construction, to improve design time and create efficient workflows by automatically detecting Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) objects from floor plans, and it is the first step towards building tools that will help energy-efficient building design.

</details>


### [56] [TRIAGE: Risk-Controlled Pseudo-Label Admission for Annotation-Efficient Semi-Supervised Retinal OCT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14321)
*Md Ashraful Hossen Akash,Shyla Afroge,Abdullah Al Mamun,Md. Kishor Morol,Tze Hui Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAGE，一个基于患者级共形风险控制器的半监督OCT扫描分类框架，采用非对称代价矩阵处理不同错误类型的不均衡影响，通过分层分类器、患者分组共形风险控制器和上下文感知Transformer教师三个模块实现仅需少量标注数据即可高效分类视网膜疾病。在Noor眼科医院数据集上，仅用20%标注数据即达89.66%扫描级准确率，优于六种SOTA半监督方法，并在OCT-C8数据集上验证了跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT作为先进视网膜疾病诊断影像技术，因专家标注成本高昂而缺乏自动化。现有半监督学习方法生成伪标签时仅基于预测置信度，未考虑不同类型错误（如欠分级与过分级）之间的不对称性，导致临床应用中可能产生严重误诊风险。

Method: TRIAGE由三个核心模块组成：1) 分层分类器，支持疾病亚型的部分异常监督；2) 患者分组的共形风险控制器，采用原始-对偶覆盖控制并集成非对称代价矩阵；3) 上下文感知Transformer教师，用于跨切片验证。该方法利用未标注B-scan数据，通过风险受控的半监督学习框架实现OCT扫描分类。

Result: 在Noor眼科医院数据集（16,822个B-scans、161名患者、554个容积）中，仅用20%标注数据达到89.66%扫描级准确率、0.8805宏F1、0.9641宏AUC和8.34%欠分级率；仅用5%标注数据保持76.88%准确率。相比六种SOTA半监督方法显著优于，欠分级率较固定阈值方法降低42.7%。在OCT-C8数据集上用1%标注数据达98.00%（3类）、10%标注数据达95.94%（8类）准确率。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过将患者级共形风险控制与非对称代价矩阵引入半监督OCT分类框架，有效解决了伪标签置信度方法忽略错误不对称性的问题，在极少标注数据下实现了优异的分类性能和低临床风险，各模块的消融实验验证了其独立贡献。

Abstract: The advanced retinal disease diagnosing imaging modality, optical coherence tomography (OCT), encounters a lack of automation because of the high expenses for annotations performed by specialists. The use of SSL solves the problem of insufficient annotations using unlabeled B-scans; however, most of the current techniques for generating pseudo-labels are based on prediction confidence without considering the asymmetry between different types of errors. This paper proposes TRIAGE, a risk-controlled semi-supervised framework for OCT scans classification, which uses the concept of a patient-level conformal risk controller with an asymmetric cost matrix. TRIAGE unites three crucial modules: a hierarchical classifier that is capable of working with partially abnormal supervision of the disease subtypes, a patient-grouped conformal risk controller with primal-dual coverage control, and a context-aware Transformer teacher for cross-slice verification. On the dataset from Noor Eye Hospital (16,822 B-scans, 161 patients, and 554 volumes) with a test set of unseen patients, TRIAGE demonstrates 89.66% scan-level accuracy, 0.8805 macro-F1, 0.9641 macro-AUC, and an 8.34% under-grading rate when using only 20% of the labeled data. With only 5% of the labeled data, TRIAGE keeps 76.88% accuracy and a 0.1656 under-grading rate. Compared with the other six state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, TRIAGE significantly outperforms them with ablation study demonstrating the contribution of each module in the overall framework performance (by 42.7% in terms of under-grading rate comparing to fixed threshold methods). TRIAGE demonstrates 98.00% accuracy for 3-class classification with 1% labeled data and 95.94% accuracy for 8-class classification with 10% labeled data on the OCT-C8 dataset.

</details>


### [57] [Weakly Supervised Polar Low Segmentation in Sentinel-1 SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14366)
*Andrea Federici,Jakob Grahn,Giacomo Boracchi,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CREST，一种基于弱监督语义分割（WSSS）的框架，仅利用图像级标签即可生成极地低压（Polar low）的像素级分割掩码。CREST基于对抗擦除（AER）方法，通过"约束有序区域扩展"（CORE）模块编码空间连通性先验，以及"动态自举"（DB）损失根据挖掘顺序调节监督强度，有效抑制了标准AER收集无关背景特征的问题，在Sentinel-1 SAR数据上比标准AER更贴近气旋结构，并在含密集标注的BUS-UCLM乳腺超声和PASCAL VOC人像数据上也表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极地低压是高速形成于高纬度地区的强烈海洋气旋，深度学习虽可在合成孔径雷达（SAR）图像中检测到它们，但像素级分割仍是一个开放挑战：缺乏可用的像素级掩码进行训练，且极地低压的范围本质上具有主观性，边界模糊，即便专家标注也不一致。因此需要一个不依赖像素级标注的弱监督分割方法。

Method: CREST框架建立在Adversarial Erasing (AER)基础上，AER通过迭代挖掘判别性区域、擦除后重新训练分类器来揭示互补线索并生成伪标签。CREST包含两大核心组件：(1) Constrained Ordinal Region Expansion (CORE)模块，编码极地低压的空间连通性先验，从高置信度种子约束区域扩张；(2) Dynamic Bootstrapping (DB)损失，将挖掘顺序视为标签可靠性代理，对噪声较大的后期挖掘区域弱化监督。

Result: 在Sentinel-1 SAR数据上，CREST比标准AER更紧密地拟合气旋结构，并输出多类别掩码（而非二值掩码），类别表示各区域的可靠性等级。在具有密集掩码的BUS-UCLM乳腺超声和PASCAL VOC人像数据上，相同设置下CREST均优于等效的AER管线。

Conclusion: CREST通过引入空间连通性先验和基于挖掘顺序的软标签策略，有效解决了标准AER在极地低压分割中收集无关背景特征的问题，仅凭图像级标签即可生成质量更高的分割掩码，并在多个满足连通性先验的数据集上验证了其泛化能力。

Abstract: Polar lows are intense maritime cyclones that form rapidly at high latitudes. Deep learning can detect them in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery, but pixel-level segmentation remains an open challenge. No pixel-level masks are available for training, and a polar low's extent is inherently subjective, with diffuse boundaries that even experts delineate inconsistently. We propose Constrained Region Erasing with Soft Targets (CREST), a Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation (WSSS) framework that generates masks solely from image-level labels. Our approach builds on Adversarial Erasing (AER), which iteratively mines discriminative regions, erases them, and retrains a classifier to reveal complementary cues that become pseudo-labels for segmentation. However, standard AER also collects irrelevant background features, degrading pseudo-label quality. CREST addresses this with (i) a Constrained Ordinal Region Expansion (CORE) module that encodes the spatial-connectedness prior of polar lows, constraining region expansion from a high-confidence seed, and (ii) a Dynamic Bootstrapping (DB) loss that treats the mining order as a proxy for label reliability, attenuating supervision from noisier, later-mined regions. On Sentinel-1 SAR data, CREST follows the cyclone structure more closely than standard AER, and returns a multi-class rather than binary mask whose classes indicate the reliability assigned to each region. We further evaluate on BUS-UCLM breast ultrasound and PASCAL VOC person data, whose targets satisfy the same connectedness prior but come with the dense masks the SAR data lacks. On both datasets, CREST performs better than the equivalent AER pipeline under identical settings.

</details>


### [58] [GBU-Palm: A Multimodal Video Dataset and Benchmark for Palm Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14389)
*Yingjie Ma,Zitong Yu,Wei Jia,Ajay Kumar,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GBU-Palm，一个大规模多模态视频掌纹呈现攻击检测（PAD）数据集与基准，包含105名受试者、21,326段视频、跨6种采集环境、RGB-NIR双模态及真伪/打印/重放三类呈现。通过泄漏受控协议分离身份与攻击血缘，评估四种代表性视频架构，发现环境迁移下性能显著退化，且RGB-NIR融合并不稳定优于纯RGB输入。进一步通过错误分解、频谱掩蔽、时间顺序干预和冻结主干NIR探测等分析，揭示了不同架构的失败模式与证据利用差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掌纹PAD数据集受限于静态图像、采集条件狭窄或缺乏多模态视频数据，无法在环境、模态和攻击类型三个维度上进行系统评估，亟需一个统一且具有挑战性的跨环境基准。

Method: 构建GBU-Palm数据集：包含21,326段视频、105名受试者、210只手掌、6种采集环境、12,010段真伪视频及6,310对同步RGB-NIR样本；设计泄漏受控协议以分离手掌身份与攻击血缘；评估四种代表性视频架构；通过真实接受/拒绝等错误分解、频谱掩蔽、时间顺序干预和冻结主干NIR探测进行行为分析。

Result: 实验显示不同架构在环境迁移下呈现显著的性能退化，RGB-NIR融合并不一致优于纯RGB输入；错误分解和干预实验揭示了各架构间不同的失败模式与证据利用策略。

Conclusion: GBU-Palm为跨环境条件下鲁棒多模态掌纹PAD方法的发展与评估提供了统一且具有挑战性的基准，填补了现有数据集在多环境中泛化评估方面的空白。

Abstract: Existing palm presentation attack detection (PAD) datasets are often limited by static imagery, restricted acquisition conditions, or insufficient multimodal video data, hindering systematic evaluation across environments, modalities, and attack types. We present GBU-Palm, a large-scale multimodal video dataset and benchmark containing 21,326 videos from 105 subjects and 210 palms across six acquisition environments, including bona fide, Print, and Replay presentations, with 6,310 synchronized RGB-NIR samples. We construct leakage-controlled protocols that separate palm identity and attack lineage and benchmark four representative video architectures under environment-matched and held-out-environment settings. Results reveal substantial architecture-dependent degradation under environmental shift and show that RGB-NIR fusion does not consistently outperform RGB-only input. We further analyze model behavior through true accept (TA), true reject (TR), false accept (FA), and false reject (FR) decomposition, spectral masking, temporal-order intervention, and frozen-backbone NIR probing, revealing distinct failure patterns and evidence utilization across architectures. GBU-Palm provides a unified and challenging benchmark for developing and evaluating robust multimodal palm PAD methods under cross-environment conditions.

</details>


### [59] [Can We Defend Against AI-Generated Video Attacks on Real-World Crisis Events? A Systematic Evaluation of Detectors, Generators and Social Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14391)
*Shuo Liang,Yixing Ma,Pengfei Zhou,Xingyan Chen,Zihan Mei,Manting Li,Feihan Chen,Zhiwen Wang,Bin Xu,Haotian Zhang,Jiajun Song,Shiya Su,Run Liu,Zhenghang Ni,Yifa Yu,Jintao Hong,Bolong Feng,Yifei Liu,Zirui Zhang,Jingxuan Zhang,Songlin Zhao,Yifan Bai,Kang Tan,Yizhe Liu,Junhao Du,Yongtao Ge,Zhaopan Xv,Xinyuan Zhang,Mengru Ma,Chunhua Shen,Wei Wang,Yang You,Zheng Zhu,Kaipeng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-Bench基准，包含17,886个视频（1,830个真实视频锚点+16,056个生成片段），横跨10个社会风险类别和9种生成器，系统评估AI生成视频检测在战争、灾难等现实危机场景中的表现。研究发现当前三类检测器均无法统一泛化，生成属性对不同检测器家族影响各异，且社会传播会使检测更加困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期视频生成器能够制造高度逼真的战争、灾难和公共紧急事件视频，带来严重的虚假信息风险。但现有基准缺乏对检测器和生成器在此类场景中行为的系统评估，包括检测能力如何随生成条件变化、人们如何感知生成视频，以及检测器在社会传播过程中是否仍然可靠。

Method: 构建RA-Bench基准：以1,830个真实视频为锚点，覆盖10个社会风险类别，使用4个开源和5个闭源生成器生成16,056个视频片段。评估沿三个维度展开：（1）检测器泛化能力，涵盖7个传统检测器、10个零样本多模态模型（3种审查设置）及2个专门微调的MLLM；（2）检测能力随生成质量、条件信息和采样种子变化的情况；（3）人类真实性判断与检测器在社会传播中的可靠性。

Result: 三类检测器家族均无法在RA-Bench实例上统一泛化。生成属性对检测器家族的影响存在差异，而源级检测模式在不同种子上保持稳定。容易误导人类的视频同样难以被当前检测器识别，社会传播进一步降低了检测难度。

Conclusion: 当前检测方法在识别逼真AI生成视频方面表现挣扎，凸显了对能够适应不断演进的视频生成器的鲁棒检测器的迫切需求。

Abstract: Recent video generators can fabricate realistic depictions of wars, disasters, public emergencies, and other real-world crises, creating substantial risks of misinformation. Existing benchmarks, however, provide limited evidence on detector and generator behavior in such settings, including how detectability varies with generation conditions, how people perceive generated videos, and whether detectors remain reliable during social dissemination. To address this gap, we introduce RA-Bench, a benchmark for AI-generated video detection that uses Real videos as Anchors. RA-Bench contains 17,886 videos, comprising 1,830 real-video anchors across 10 social-risk categories and 16,056 generated clips from four open-source and five closed-source generators. Based on RA-Bench, we organize our evaluation along three dimensions. We first assess detector generalization across seven traditional detectors, ten zero-shot multimodal models under three review settings, and two MLLMs specifically fine-tuned on AI-generated video detection. Across these methods, none of the three detector families generalizes consistently across RA-Bench instances. We then examine how detectability varies with generation quality, conditioning information, and sampling seeds. These analyses show that generation properties affect detector families differently, while source-level detection patterns remain stable across seeds. Finally, we study human authenticity judgments and detector reliability during social dissemination. We find that videos that mislead people are also difficult for current detectors, and that social dissemination makes detection harder. Together, these findings show that current methods struggle to detect realistic AI-generated videos, highlighting the need for detectors robust to evolving video generators.

</details>


### [60] [IRGNN: Efficient Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for Radar Point Cloud Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14394)
*Xiao Guo,Wanke Xia,Lili Yang,Caicong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IRGNN，一种用于雷达点云目标检测的不变雷达图神经网络。IRGNN将雷达点云重构为具有平移和旋转不变性的图表示，通过改进的消息传递神经网络（含残差连接和虚拟节点层）增强特征传播和全局建模，最终在RadarScenes数据集上取得了优于现有雷达检测方法的性能，同时显著降低了推理时的计算成本和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR-based目标检测方法在恶劣天气下可靠性下降，而雷达点云虽对恶劣天气和低光照场景具有鲁棒性，但其稀疏、无序和信息量低的特点使得现有LiDAR检测方法难以直接应用。因此需要专门针对雷达点云特性设计高效的目标检测方法。

Method: IRGNN包含三个核心步骤：(1) 利用平移和旋转不变的特征设计，将雷达点云重构为图表示，以鲁棒地建模稀疏雷达测量；(2) 采用改进的消息传递神经网络（MPNN），引入残差连接和虚拟节点层，增强局部特征传播和全局上下文建模；(3) 对学习到的图表示应用任务特定的头部网络，进行目标分类和边界框预测。

Result: 在RadarScenes数据集上的实验表明，IRGNN优于现有基于雷达的目标检测方法，取得了具有竞争力的性能。此外，IRGNN在推理阶段的计算成本和内存占用显著降低，展示了其在自动驾驶中高效雷达感知方面的实际潜力。

Conclusion: IRGNN通过图神经网络与不变特征设计的结合，有效解决了雷达点云稀疏、无序带来的挑战，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡，为自动驾驶中恶劣天气下的鲁棒感知提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Perception is a fundamental component of autonomous driving systems. While LiDAR-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in object detection, their reliability can degrade under adverse weather conditions. Radar point clouds provide a robust alternative due to their resilience to bad weather and low-illumination scenarios. However, radar point clouds are typically sparse, unordered, and less informative than LiDAR data, making it challenging to directly apply existing LiDAR-based perception methods. To address these challenges, we propose IRGNN, an Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for radar point cloud object detection. IRGNN first reconstructs radar point clouds into graph representations using translation- and rotation-invariant feature designs, enabling robust modeling of sparse radar measurements. It then employs an improved message passing neural network (MPNN) with residual connections and a virtual node layer to enhance local feature propagation and global context modeling. Finally, task-specific heads are applied to the learned graph representations for object classification and bounding box prediction. Experimental results on the RadarScenes dataset show that IRGNN outperforms existing radar-based object detection methods and achieves competitive performance. In addition, IRGNN significantly reduces computational cost and memory usage during inference, demonstrating its effectiveness and practical potential for efficient radar-based perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [61] [CRAFT: Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning for Subject Personalization without Composed Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14403)
*Jihun Park,Kyoungmin Lee,Jongmin Gim,Hyeonseo Jo,Jaeyeul Kim,Han Zou,Zhenpeng Zhan,Yan Zhang,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT框架，利用仅10K张参考图（无合成目标监督）通过注意力级奖励微调预训练的参考感知多模态扩散Transformer（MMDiT），在FLUX.2-klein-9B上取得SOTA性能，且可迁移至其他参考感知骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动图像个性化方法依赖大规模（数十万至数百万）配对的（参考，合成目标）数据，其生成需多阶段且昂贵的策展流程（LLM提示生成、T2I合成、参考主体提取、VLM质量过滤和对应标注），且方法与特定的目标合成器和策展选择紧密耦合，成本高且泛化受限。

Method: CRAFT采用单步ReFL框架，通过LoRA适配器对预训练的参考感知MMDiT进行微调。其核心是"Where to look"原则：注意力级奖励使噪声token与短语token的注意力对齐到正确的参考主体，生成的逐主体注意力掩码进一步门控像素级身份奖励，确保图像空间监督与学习到的注意力路由一致。仅需10K参考图像及主体掩码，无需合成目标监督。

Result: 在FLUX.2-klein-9B上，CRAFT在XVerseBench基准上达到SOTA性能，且仅使用10K参考样本（无合成目标监督），而此前广义方法需150K至超过2M的合成目标对。该配方可迁移至其他参考感知骨干网络，均一致提升性能。

Conclusion: CRAFT证明了通过精心设计的注意力级奖励与掩码门控的像素级奖励，可以在极小规模参考数据监督下实现与大规模数据策展方法相当甚至更优的个性化生成效果，显著降低数据构建成本并提高方法可迁移性。

Abstract: Subject-driven image personalization---generating new images that preserve the identity of one or several reference subjects in novel scenes---is a foundational capability for modern visual content creation. It is currently dominated by generalized methods that fine-tune a pretrained multimodal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) on hundreds of thousands to millions of paired \emph{(reference, composed-target)} examples, where each composed target is a synthesized image of the subject in a novel scene. Producing such targets demands a costly multi-stage curation pipeline---LLM-based prompt generation, T2I-based composed-target synthesis, reference-subject extraction, VLM-based quality filtering, and correspondence labeling---and tightly couples each method to a particular target synthesizer and curation choice. We introduce \emph{CRAFT} (Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning), a single-step ReFL framework that fine-tunes a pre-trained \emph{reference-aware} MMDiT via LoRA adapters using a compact reference-only data construction---$10$K reference images and subject masks, with no composed-target supervision. CRAFT realizes a \emph{Where to look} principle: attention-level rewards align noise- and phrase-token attention with the correct reference subject, and the resulting per-subject attention masks gate a pixel-level identity reward to keep image-space supervision consistent with the learned attention routing. Applied to FLUX.2-klein-9B, CRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on XVerseBench \rev{while using no composed-target supervision---only $10$K reference-only samples, whereas prior generalized methods require $150$K to over $2$M composed-target pairs}. The same recipe transfers to other reference-aware backbones, consistently improving performance. Project page: https://jihun999.github.io/projects/CRAFT/.

</details>


### [62] [GhostPoint: Self-Supervised Representation Learning by Hallucinating Occluded LiDAR Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14428)
*Mohamed Abdelsamad,Bin Yang,Michael Ulrich,Miao Zhang,Yakov Miron,Alexandru Paul Condurache,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GhostPoint，一个用于LiDAR点云3D目标检测的自监督学习框架，通过实例体素膨胀在局部邻域中生成幻觉潜在特征，解决现有SSL方法仅关注可见表面导致的目标检测迁移性能弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法的目标函数仅定义在测量到的LiDAR可见表面上，忽略了遮挡和未观测区域，导致可见表面偏差。这种偏差对逐点任务（如语义/全景分割）足够，但3D目标检测需要应对缺失结构的鲁棒性，因此需要额外建模可见表面之外的结构信息。

Method: GhostPoint框架包含：（1）实例体素膨胀——在检测到的实例周围邻域生成幻觉潜在特征；（2）编码器处理观测返回，额外的预测器从观测上下文推断邻域表示；（3）双重监督——标准编码器级监督（观测/掩码体素匹配教师编码器目标）和预测器级监督（未观测体素匹配教师预测器的幻觉输出），使表征显式建模超出可见返回的结构。

Result: 在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上的大量实验表明，GhostPoint达到最先进的性能，一致地提升下游3D目标检测，尤其在稀疏扫描和少量标注数据场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: 通过引入预测器级监督和实例级别的邻域特征幻觉，GhostPoint有效弥补了SSL中可见表面偏差的问题，为3D目标检测提供更鲁棒的表征，验证了在低密度点云和有限标注条件下自监督预训练的实际价值。

Abstract: 3D object detection from LiDAR point clouds is a core problem in autonomous driving. Recent advances in self-supervised learning (SSL) enable scalable pretraining and transfers well to per-point tasks such as semantic and panoptic segmentation, but transfer to 3D detection remains weaker. We analyze recent SSL methods and find that most objectives are defined only on measured LiDAR returns from visible surfaces, leaving occluded and unobserved regions unconstrained. This visible-surface bias can be sufficient for point-wise prediction, but 3D detection requires robustness to missing structure. To address this gap, we propose GhostPoint, an SSL framework that hallucinates latent features in local neighborhoods around discovered instances, generated via a novel instance voxel dilation. In GhostPoint, an encoder processes observed returns, and an additional predictor infers neighborhood representations from observed context. In addition to standard encoder-level supervision, we introduce a predictor-level supervision scheme on sampled voxels from generated neighborhoods. Specifically, observed (visible/masked) voxels match teacher-encoder targets, while unobserved voxels match teacher-predictor hallucinations. This design encourages the learned representation to explicitly model structure beyond observed returns. Extensive evaluations on nuScenes and Waymo demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently improving downstream 3D detection, especially under sparse scans and limited labels.

</details>


### [63] [Style or Signature? Artist-Disjoint Evaluation of Style Classification in Frozen Vision Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14435)
*Rory Ashton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了使用CLIP等冻结图像嵌入进行绘画风格分类时，报告的高准确率可能源于对个别艺术家的识别而非真正的风格理解。通过艺术家不重叠（artist-disjoint）的评估协议，排除同一艺术家作品同时出现在训练和测试集的情况后，5近邻风格分类准确率从0.87降至0.77，且下降极不均衡：印象派和立体派几乎不受影响，而超现实主义下降20个百分点。该模式在四种图像编码器上一致，表明这源于视觉结构而非语言信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用随机划分评估冻结图像嵌入的风格分类准确率，但随机划分中同一艺术家的作品会同时出现在训练和测试两侧，模型可通过识别画家身份而非风格流派来取得高分。作者质疑这种高准确率是否真反映了风格理解，提出需要在艺术家不重叠协议下重新评估。

Method: 采用艺术家逐一排除（artist-disjoint）的评估协议，即依次将每位艺术家从训练集中完全排除，确保某作品被分类时不会用到该艺术家的其他作品作为参考。在包含320幅画作、横跨20世纪四个运动（印象派、立体派、超现实主义等）的平衡数据集上，使用5近邻分类器进行风格分类，并在四种图像编码器（包括仅视觉的自监督模型）上进行验证。

Result: 在艺术家不重叠协议下，5近邻风格分类准确率从0.87降至0.77，下降分布极不均衡。印象派和立体派的准确率几乎不变，而超现实主义下降20个百分点。该模式在四种图像编码器中一致出现，包括不依赖语言信息的纯视觉自监督模型，表明问题源于视觉结构而非文本偏差。当编码器捕捉到真正共享的视觉形式时，个体艺术家难以被识别但风格识别保持稳健，而超现实主义则呈现相反特征。

Conclusion: 冻结嵌入中的风格分类高准确率可能被艺术家身份识别所混淆，艺术家不重叠评估是衡量风格理解所必需的协议。研究者应采用此评估方式以避免高估嵌入模型对艺术风格的真正理解能力。

Abstract: Frozen image embeddings from models such as CLIP are increasingly used to classify paintings by art-historical style, with high reported accuracy. We ask whether this accuracy reflects an understanding of style or the recognition of individual artists. Standard evaluation uses random splits in which works by the same artist appear on both sides, so a classifier can succeed by recognising the painter rather than the movement. We re-evaluate style classification under an artist-disjoint protocol, holding out every artist in turn so that no work is ever classified using other works by its own painter. On a balanced dataset of 320 paintings across four twentieth-century movements, 5-NN style accuracy falls from 0.87 to 0.77 under this protocol, and the drop is sharply uneven. Impressionism and Cubism barely move, while Surrealism falls twenty points. The pattern holds across four image encoders, including a vision-only self-supervised model, which places the effect in visual structure rather than language. Where an encoder captures genuine shared form, individual artists are barely recognisable yet style is robust, while Surrealism shows the opposite. We argue that artist-disjoint evaluation is necessary to measure stylistic understanding in frozen embeddings.

</details>


### [64] [Marionette: Predicting World States, Rendering Geometry, Painting Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14530)
*Zian Meng,Zhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Qiang Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Marionette，一种用于交互式游戏的世界模型。它将世界状态的几何计算显式建模并委托给固定的零参数渲染器，而神经网络仅负责外观合成。该模型由三部分组成：两阶段自回归动力学模型预测276维的显式3D世界状态（多实体关节骨架、度量根轨迹和旋转）；零参数图形桥将预测状态转换为姿态控制视频，以闭式解计算几何和遮挡；控制条件视频扩散观察模型合成逼真的RGB观察。实验表明其可直接控制且长时行为稳定可修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式游戏世界模型直接在像素或潜空间自回归地预测视觉观察，迫使姿态、几何和遮挡等结构化属性由同一个生成序列隐式维护。在长时程上，这些潜在世界属性的误差会累积，导致一致性和可控性变得脆弱。作者旨在通过显式建模世界状态来解决这一问题。

Method: Marionette采用三阶段架构：(1) 两阶段自回归动力学模型预测显式的276维3D世界状态，包含多实体关节骨架、度量根轨迹和旋转；(2) 零参数图形渲染桥将预测状态转换为姿态控制视频，以闭式方式计算世界空间几何与遮挡；(3) 控制条件的视频扩散观察模型从结构化控制生成逼真的RGB观察。这种设计将精确几何计算委托给固定渲染器，让神经模型仅负责外观合成。

Result: 实验验证了两个关键性质：(1) 预测的世界状态可直接控制——强制不匹配的动作流使根对齐关节误差改变31%（48个保留段）；(2) 长时程行为由状态决定并可在状态中修复——自由生成时两个角色漂移至相距21.2米（记录会话保持约5米），且三分之一的帧出现地面穿透；施加地形碰撞器和分离上限两条规则可将穿透减少66%并保持角色互动距离，无需修改观察模型。外观保真度方面，FVD为831，对比记录姿态的799，未检测到明显损失。

Conclusion: Marionette证明了将世界状态的几何计算显式化并委托给零参数渲染器是一种有效的范式，它使世界模型同时具备可解释性、可控制性和长时程稳定性，且外观合成保真度几乎不受影响。未来的研究方向包括处理更丰富的物体类别、开放世界以及更复杂的交互。

Abstract: Interactive game world models typically autoregress visual observations directly in pixel or latent space, forcing structured properties such as pose, geometry, and occlusion to be implicitly maintained by the same generative sequence. Over long horizons, errors in these latent world properties accumulate, making consistency and controllability fragile. We explicitly model the evolving world state, delegate exact geometric computation to a fixed, zero-parameter renderer, and leave the neural model to synthesize appearance. We instantiate this idea as Marionette, a world model for interactive games with articulated characters. First, a two-stage autoregressive dynamics model predicts an explicit and interpretable 276-dimensional 3D world state comprising multi-entity articulated skeletons, metric root trajectories, and rotations. Second, a zero-parameter graphics bridge converts the predicted state into pose-control videos, computing world-space geometry and occlusion in closed form. Third, a control-conditioned video-diffusion observation model synthesizes photorealistic RGB observations from the resulting structured controls. Our experiments establish two properties of Marionette. First, the predicted world state is directly controllable. Forcing a mismatched action stream changes root-aligned joint error by 31% across 48 held-out segments. Second, long-horizon behaviour is determined in the state, and can be repaired there. Left free, the two generated characters drift to 21.2 m apart (recorded sessions stay near 5 m) and a third of frames show ground penetration. Two rules imposed on the explicit state, a terrain collider and a separation cap, cut penetration by 66% and keep the pair engaged, with no change to the observation model. Routing appearance through the predicted state costs no fidelity we can detect, at an FVD of 831 against 799 for recorded pose.

</details>


### [65] [Decoding the Past: An Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning Framework for Sex Attribution in Prehistoric Hand Stencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539)
*Karel Becerra,Boris Mederos,Dean Snow,Ramón A. Mollineda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不确定性感知的深度学习框架，用于旧石器时代手印模板的性别归因，通过显式建模、传播和聚合不确定性，结合双图像处理、双重轮廓提取、结构化剪影增强、多模型集成及三角验证方案，在当代数据上准确率超过88%，并可输出性别预测和置信度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧石器时代手印模板的性别确定极其困难，主要源于缺乏真实标注（ground truth）、当代与史前群体的种群差异、图像退化带来的不确定性，以及传统形态测量方法在跨性别和跨种群上的高结构性重叠、泛化性差和主观特征工程等局限。

Method: 该框架包含五个核心组件：(1) 双图像处理与双重轮廓提取；(2) 结构化剪影增强，每个模板生成十二种可能的轮廓实现以捕捉边界不确定性；(3) 模型架构多样性，使用EfficientNet-B3和MobileViT-S两个集成（各含10个深度神经网络）；(4) 基于14,036个当代手部样本训练；(5) 三角验证方案，结合集成预测、无监督2D潜空间流形映射（UMAP+k-NN）以及可解释AI空间归因（LayerCAM）以确保解剖学一致性。

Result: 在当代数据上，集成模型在老年群体中准确率超过88%；应用于史前模板时，框架不仅输出性别预测，还提供内部一致性置信度度量，能够区分形态稳定与不确定的案例。集成预测、潜空间结构和可解释性分析的收敛表明不确定性可成为考古推断的可测量组成部分。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，通过将不确定性作为一种显式建模和可量化的组件纳入深度学习管道，能够实现对古代岩画的稳健且可复现的性别解码，为考古学推断提供了新的方法论范式。

Abstract: Determining the biological sex of the individuals who created Upper Paleolithic hand stencils remains a challenging problem due to the absence of ground truth, population differences between contemporary and prehistoric groups, and the uncertainty introduced by image degradation. Traditional morphometric methods suffer from high structural overlap across sexes, poor cross-population generalizability, and subjective feature engineering. This study presents an uncertainty-aware deep learning framework for sex attribution in prehistoric hand stencils that explicitly models, propagates, and aggregates uncertainty throughout the analytical pipeline. The methodology combines dual image processing, dual contour extraction, structured silhouette augmentation, model architectural diversity, and ensemble-based decision aggregation. The pipeline generates twelve plausible silhouette realizations per stencil to capture boundary uncertainties, which are processed by two ensembles of ten deep neural networks each (EfficientNet-B3 and MobileViT-S) trained on 14,036 contemporary hand samples. Furthermore, a triangulated validation scheme integrates ensemble predictions with unsupervised 2D latent-space manifold mapping (UMAP + k-NN) and explainable AI spatial attributions (LayerCAM) to ensure anatomical consistency. On contemporary data, ensemble models achieve strong classification performance, with accuracies exceeding 88% in older age groups. When applied to prehistoric stencils, the framework produces both sex predictions and confidence measures of internal agreement, enabling the distinction between morphologically stable and ambiguous cases. Convergence across ensemble predictions, latent-space structure, and interpretability analyses shows that uncertainty can become a measurable component of archaeological inference, enabling robust and reproducible decoding of ancient rock art.

</details>


### [66] [MagnifiQ: Patch-aware Text Guided Progressive Upscaling for High-Resolution Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14543)
*Mahesh Reddy,Yashesh Savani,Antoine Mercier,Hong Cai,Fatih Porikli,Guillaume Berger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagnifiQ 是一个基于扩散模型的图像恢复框架，通过将自注意力层替换为卷积操作（计算成本随分辨率线性增长）并结合渐进式上采样策略，实现从1024x1024到4096x4096的高清图像恢复，利用SDXL预训练模型和patch级文本提示增强局部细节与全局一致性，在感知质量和人类偏好上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率（4K）图像恢复面临双重挑战：既要保持全局结构一致性，又要恢复细粒度局部细节。直接应用扩散模型进行4K恢复计算开销极大，且容易产生重复或不一致的纹理伪影，需要更高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 1）将SDXL的自注意力层替换为卷积操作，使计算成本随分辨率线性增长而非二次增长；2）提出渐进式上采样策略，分多个分辨率阶段迭代恢复，逐步细化中间输出而非直接生成最终4K图像；3）使用patch特定文本提示提供空间局部化的语义引导，增强局部细节同时控制内容漂移。

Result: 在合成和真实世界退化图像上的大量实验表明，MagnifiQ在感知质量和人类偏好上优于现有的基于扩散的图像恢复方法，产生更锐利的纹理和更连贯的4K结果，并通过其可扩展骨干网络和渐进式设计提供了实用的速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: MagnifiQ通过架构级优化（卷积替换自注意力）和训练/推理策略级创新（渐进式上采样与patch级文本引导）有效解决了4K扩散图像恢复的计算瓶颈和一致性难题，展示了在保持高质量输出的同时实现高分辨率可扩展恢复的可行路径。

Abstract: High-resolution image restoration from degraded inputs is challenging because it must preserve global structural consistency while recovering fine-grained local details, especially at 4K resolution where direct diffusion-based restoration is computationally expensive and prone to repeated or inconsistent textures. In this work, we introduce MagnifiQ, an image restoration framework that progressively upscales and restores images across resolutions, e.g., from 1024x1024 to 4096x4096. Our approach leverages a pre-trained text-to-image diffusion model such as SDXL and adapts it for more scalable high-resolution inference by replacing its original self-attention layers with convolutional operations whose computational cost grows linearly with image resolution. We further propose a progressive upscaling strategy that iteratively restores images over multiple resolution stages, refining each intermediate output rather than directly hallucinating the final 4K image, thereby improving global coherence and reducing high-resolution artifacts. To enhance local details while controlling content drift, MagnifiQ uses patch-specific text prompts that provide spatially localized semantic guidance during restoration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world degraded images show that MagnifiQ outperforms prior diffusion-based restoration methods in perceptual quality and human preference, producing sharper textures and more coherent 4K results while offering practical speed--quality trade-offs through its scalable backbone and progressive design.

</details>


### [67] [CPI-Bench: A Comprehensive,Practical and Intelligent Benchmark for Real-World Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14546)
*Qinye Zhou,Jun Zheng,Yongchao Du,Yuan Wang,Zhengrui Chen,Zuan Gao,Taihang Hu,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Zhengtao Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Denghui Yang,Yuhang Yu,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPI-Bench，一个面向真实世界图像编辑的综合、实用且智能的基准测试框架，包含三个子集（General/Practical/Intelligent），覆盖多图像编辑、高频用户场景和高级推理编辑任务，并通过实验证明其能更好地区分模型性能，且与人类评估排行榜高度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准局限于简单的单图像任务，覆盖维度有限，无法有效区分不同模型的性能，难以评估模型在复杂多图像编辑、高推理需求指令以及实际部署场景中的表现，因而不能可靠地反映模型在实际应用中的能力。

Method: 提出CPI-Bench基准框架，包含三个核心子集：CPI-General-Bench（全面覆盖多种编辑任务并率先纳入多图像编辑评测）、CPI-Practical-Bench（聚焦高频真实用户应用场景）、CPI-Intelligent-Bench（专注高推理需求编辑能力评估）。

Result: 基于CPI-Bench对主流图像编辑模型的评估表明，该基准增强了模型间的性能区分度，能够全面可靠地量化模型在通用编辑能力、实际部署效果和高级推理编辑方面的差距。排名分析显示，CPI-Bench与Arena Image Edit Leaderboard实现最高对齐，忠实反映人类评估者的偏好与感知判断。

Conclusion: CPI-Bench为图像编辑模型的优化提供有价值的指导，可作为真实用户体验的可靠代理指标，弥补了现有基准在评估全面性和区分度上的不足。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of image editing models and their widespread application across various domains, there is an increasingly urgent need to deploy these model capabilities directly into real-world scenarios. However, existing benchmarks remain confined to simple single-image tasks, suffering from limited coverage dimensions and an inability to effectively differentiate performance among diverse models. Consequently, they fail to reliably evaluate model performance in complex multi-image editing, highly demanding reasoning instructions, and practical deployment settings. To address these limitations, we propose CPI-Bench, a Comprehensive, Practical andIntelligent benchmark for real-world image editing. CPI-Bench comprises three core subsets: CPI-General-Bench, which comprehensively covers diverse editing tasks and pioneers the inclusion of multi-image editing evaluation; CPI-Practical-Bench, which focuses on high-frequency real-user application scenarios; and CPI-Intelligent-Bench, which is dedicated to evaluating capabilities in highly demanding reasoning-based editing. Evaluation results of mainstream image editing models based on CPI-Bench demonstrate that CPI-Bench enhances performance differentiation among models. It provides a comprehensive and reliable quantification of gaps in general editing capabilities, practical deployment efficacy, and advanced reasoning-based editing, offering invaluable guidance for the future optimization of image editing models. Crucially, our ranking analysis reveals that CPI-Bench achieves the highest alignment with the Arena Image Edit Leaderboard, indicating it faithfully captures the preferences and perceptual judgments of human evaluators, serving as a robust proxy for real-world user experience.

</details>

### [68] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出洛伦兹傅里叶神经算子（L-FNO），一种将FNO风格的协变量路径、洛伦兹谱核用于历史依赖激发以及基于似然的训练目标相结合的随机神经算子，用于对稀疏事件流（如疾病爆发和半导体故障）建模。在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集上，L-FNO相较回归式和基于似然的神经算子基线在事件似然、校准诊断和稀有事件检测上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代运行系统在常规条件下也面临不确定性，稀有、突发且自激的事件同时受外生协变量和内生事件动力学影响。标准神经算子通常被训练为回归式的函数到函数映射模型，而非条件强度估计器，限制了其在稀疏事件场景中的适用性。

Method: L-FNO由三部分组成：(1) FNO风格的协变量路径用于处理外生输入；(2) 洛伦兹谱核实现历史依赖的激励（即事件的自激/激发机制）；(3) 基于似然的训练目标（而非回归目标）用于优化模型。整体构成一个随机神经算子，可输出条件强度函数。

Result: 在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集（涵盖疾病爆发预测和半导体故障/缺陷检测）上，L-FNO在事件似然、校准诊断和稀有事件检测方面均优于回归式和基于似然的神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 结构化谱记忆与基于似然的学习为神经算子模型在随机事件动力学中提供了有效的归纳偏置，表明神经算子可以被有效设计为条件强度估计器。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [69] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，将少量编码基准（如SWE-bench、LiveCodeBench）上的得分视为广泛编码能力的证据存在"意义鸿沟"。作者通过Django案例研究基准套件发现，在SWE-bench上优化的模型在跨任务泛化上表现不佳，基准排名往往无法推广。他们建议采用差异化评估策略，并呼吁建立能力分类体系和持续的基准维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践过度依赖少量编码基准来衡量模型的编码能力，导致基准优化产生"指标游戏化"问题——模型在特定基准上表现好，但对一般编码任务的真实能力被高估。这种基准得分与通用编码能力声明之间的意义鸿沟，会使研究人员和工程师基于不充分证据做出错误决策。

Method: 作者创建了一个基于Django框架的案例研究基准套件，对在SWE-bench轨迹上后训练的基座模型和检查点进行系统评估。他们比较了这些模型在自建基准、LiveCodeBench以及其他任务上的表现，并检验了跨任务迁移效果，同时考察了对单个Django模态进行微调后的迁移能力。

Result: 研究发现：基准排名经常无法泛化；后训练的检查点在跨任务迁移上表现微弱；SWE-bench优化在自建任务和LiveCodeBench上带来的提升有限甚至没有；针对单个Django模态的微调同样无法实现跨模态迁移。

Conclusion: 少量基准不足以在基准优化压力下评估多样化模型。社区应采用差异化评估策略：前沿模型用整体评估，研究场景用多任务套件，窄任务应用用人在回路的实验。同时需要建立能力分类体系和持续的基准维护机制，而非一次性发布基准。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [70] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了XGBoost对异常值（垂直离群点和杠杆点）的鲁棒性，并提出基于M-, S-, τ-估计器的替代损失函数，其中MM-XGBoost两步法在鲁棒性和预测精度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost是非常流行的预测方法，但其标准损失函数（二次损失）在面对垂直离群点和杠杆点等异常值时的性能可能受到影响，缺乏系统的鲁棒性研究。

Method: 探索基于鲁棒回归中M-, S-, τ-估计器的替代损失函数，并特别提出一种两步法MM-XGBoost，兼顾鲁棒性与预测精度。

Result: 实验结果表明MM-XGBoost在鲁棒性和预测精度之间提供了最佳权衡。

Conclusion: MM-XGBoost是增强XGBoost鲁棒性的有效方案，可作为标准XGBoost在含异常值场景下的更稳健替代方法。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [71] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Activation-Prune-Merge (APM)框架，解决跨规模异构模型融合问题——即如何将更强的"供体"大模型的能力迁移到较小的"受体"模型中，无需显式的神经元级语义对齐。APM通过激活图引导的方式裁剪供体模型至受体架构，并以极小的混合系数注入受体，在16个基准上将3B受体的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构模型融合研究主要关注任务、初始化、架构或规模等维度的差异，但跨规模融合（将大模型能力迁移至小模型）尚未被充分探索。传统方法通常依赖神经元级语义对齐，而跨规模且架构不匹配的模型难以进行精确的逐神经元对应。作者希望验证：在供体贡献足够集中且经过仔细选择的情况下，无需显式语义对齐即可实现跨规模能力迁移。

Method: APM框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 激活引导的供体分析——在供体上构建任务条件化的激活映射；(2) 裁剪——根据激活图选择重要的层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元，将大供体裁剪为与受体匹配的小架构；(3) 合并——以微小的插值系数将裁剪后的供体切片注入原始受体模型。该方法将供体视为"集中功能组件"的来源，而非要求精确的结构移植。

Result: 在16个涵盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令遵循和分类的基准上，APM将3B受体的整体平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。具体任务中，RTE准确率从64.3%提升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%提升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%提升至79.2%。对注入比例和序贯多阶段融合的分析进一步表明，激活引导的提取能提高可迁移供体切片的质量，同时保持小比例融合机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 跨规模异构模型融合可以在无需显式语义对齐的情况下成功实现，前提是供体的贡献足够集中并经过精心选择。APM通过激活引导的裁剪-合并策略证明了这一观点，为模型融合提供了新的方法论路径，并开放了无需复杂对齐机制的轻量级跨模型知识迁移方向。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [72] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过配对种子、多种噪声结构和预算的系统性实验，检验了不确定性采样在主动学习中的鲁棒性，发现其标签效率优势因数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标而异，在干净标签下提升0.09-1.77个百分点，但困难依赖噪声比随机分类噪声更具破坏性仅在某些数据集上成立，没有证据表明错误位置的结构化分布会带来普遍性额外惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性采样是主动学习中常用策略，但其倾向于选择最难样本可能导致标签错误增多。现有研究对不确定性采样是否因获取更多损坏标签而失效，或因其错误集中在困难区域而特别有害这一问题缺乏系统性的因果分离验证。

Method: 在三个公开二分类表格数据集上，对比边界不确定性采样与随机采样，涵盖干净标签、随机分类噪声(RCN)和界限依赖难度噪声三种条件。使用100个配对种子、0-0.30的9个期望噪声率、20-120的标注预算，逻辑回归在每个预算下通过交叉验证重新选择正则化。设计暴露匹配的RCN对照组以对齐最终平均获取损坏率，并扩展干净标签实验至预算400。

Result: 干净标签下不确定性采样在所有数据集上提升了归一化平衡准确率曲线下面积1.09-1.77个百分点。难度依赖噪声在乳腺癌数据集的8个噪声率中的6个上比RCN更有削弱作用，但在其他两个数据集上无此现象。暴露匹配分析未发现错误位置的结构化布局带来额外惩罚的一致证据。在干净MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提高平衡准确率的同时降低了平均精度和固定假阳率下的真阳率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样总体上是标签高效的，但其表观鲁棒性并非普适，关键取决于数据集特性、标注预算、噪声结构及评价指标的选择。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [73] [High-dimensional nonparametric changepoint detection via low-rank degree-two density projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13922)
*Guoqing Zhang,Zhaixin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于表示学习的高维分布变化检测方法，通过构造对称特征矩阵进行秩截断后的矩阵CUSUM扫描，替代非参数密度估计，实现无需参数假设的快速变化点检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分布变化检测在前后密度均无参数化假设时极具挑战性。传统方法依赖密度估计或个体坐标稀疏性假设，难以应对高维、非参数、且变化方向低秩而非坐标稀疏的场景。

Method: 构建对称特征矩阵H2(X)，使其期望M(f)成为密度二阶正交投影的等距编码；对矩阵CUSUM进行秩r截断后扫描（LRD估计器），利用投影跳跃的低秩性而非坐标稀疏性；引入seeded narrowest-over-threshold过程处理多重变化并通过保持孤立区间的归纳法证明精确恢复；采用交叉拟合标量细化在折叠间学习变化低秩方向并定位，结合依赖矩阵Bernstein不等式扩展到几何β混合序列。

Result: 非渐近算子范数分析中主随机项以√(rd log(nd))为标度；交叉拟合细化达到Õ(κ⁻²)误差，并匹配Le Cam下界（至多对数因子），证明最优性；实验在维度高达200、三变化d=100序列及128特征人体活动基准上验证了计算可行性和对纯依赖变化的准确检测。

Conclusion: 该方法通过矩阵均值估计替代密度估计、利用低秩结构替代稀疏假设，在理论上达到对数最优，实践上能检测均值CUSUM无法捕捉的纯依赖结构变化，具有高维下高效且可扩展的优点。

Abstract: Detecting distributional changes in high dimension is difficult when neither the pre-change nor post-change density is parametrically specified. We introduce a representation-based approach that retains all degree-at-most-two density information while replacing density estimation by matrix mean estimation. For observations in $[-1,1]^d$, a symmetric feature matrix $H_2(X)\in\R^{(d+1)\times(d+1)}$ is constructed so that $M(f)=\E_f H_2(X)$ is an isometric encoding of the degree-two orthogonal projection of the density. We scan matrix CUSUMs after rank-$r$ truncation, exploiting the low rank of the projected jump rather than sparsity of individual coordinates. The resulting \LRD{} estimator has a tent-shaped population objective and a nonasymptotic operator-norm analysis whose leading stochastic term scales as $\sqrt{rd\log(nd)}$. For multiple changes, we give a seeded narrowest-over-threshold procedure and prove exact recovery by an induction that preserves an isolating interval for every undetected change. A cross-fitted scalar refinement learns the changing low-rank direction on one fold and localizes on the other, attaining $\widetilde O_{\Pp}(κ^{-2})$ error; a matching Le Cam lower bound shows optimality up to logarithms. A geometrically $β$-mixing extension follows from a dependent matrix Bernstein inequality. Experiments with ambient dimension up to $200$, a three-change $d=100$ sequence, and a $128$-feature human-activity benchmark show that the method remains computationally practical and accurately detects pure dependence changes that are invisible to mean CUSUMs.

</details>


### [74] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于核风险敏感均值p次幂准则的随机向量函数链接（KRPRVFL）网络模型，通过替换传统最小二乘目标函数为KRP损失，解决RVFL对噪声标签、离群值和数据不平衡敏感的问题，在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上表现出更优的准确性、鲁棒性和统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL网络虽然具有轻量、快速、强泛化等优势，但对噪声标签、离群值和类不平衡数据高度敏感，严重限制了其在真实场景中的应用性能。现有方法缺乏对此类问题的有效鲁棒性保障，亟需一种既能保持RVFL计算效率又能增强抗干扰能力的模型。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心设计：（1）以核风险敏感均值p次幂（KRP）准则替代标准最小二乘目标函数，自适应降低损坏或不可靠样本在训练中的影响；（2）引入协作学习机制，实现模型组件间的自适应交互以增强复杂噪声环境下的鲁棒性；（3）利用核诱导特征映射捕获非线性关系，无需显式的隐层选择，兼顾效率与可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确性、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面均一致优于基线模型，验证了其作为快速、可扩展且可靠的分类解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL通过融合RVFL的计算效率和KRP准则的鲁棒性，成功解决了传统RVFL在噪声和复杂环境下的局限性，提供了一种兼具准确性、鲁棒性和效率的可靠分类工具，适合处理真实世界中的挑战性分类任务。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [75] [QUASAR: Lowering the Loss Floor of Quantization-Aware Training with Loss-Aware Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13966)
*Vincent Counathe,Ben Athiwaratkun,Christopher De Sa,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUASAR是一种量化感知训练（QAT）方法，通过在训练循环中持续进行轻量级的、损失感知的重建来降低损失下限并改善最终低比特模型质量。它在Qwen3和Llama-3.1上于2/3/4比特场景下取得了最优的KL散度，在2比特下将八项任务的平均准确率提升了3.5-4.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理向更低精度迁移，后训练量化（PTQ）变得愈发脆弱，量化感知训练（QAT）成为保质量的关键。然而现有QAT存在根本性失配：损失和代理梯度基于有损重建的全精度权重计算，而更新却应用于潜在全精度权重本身，导致次优训练轨迹和更高的损失下限。二阶PTQ方法虽能缓解类似问题，但仅对冻结模型做一次就要数小时，在QAT过程中重复此流程不切实际。

Method: QUASAR在每次训练步骤中：(1) 使用平方梯度的指数移动平均作为在线显著性估计；(2) 在小范围候选集上搜索最优裁剪范围；(3) 通过显著性加权的 least squares 拟合仿射反量化器。理论分析证明损失感知的重建误差是QAT收敛界中唯一与重建相关的项，并控制最终量化模型的损失。QUASAR仅修改训练流程，支持标准部署格式（整数量化、NVFP4），无推理时改动或开销。

Result: 在Qwen3和Llama-3.1上，QUASAR在2/3/4比特下取得最低的留出KL散度，3/4比特下KL降低至少10%，2比特下降低29%；在2比特时，八项任务的平均准确率比强QAT和PTQ基线提升3.5-4.3个百分点。

Conclusion: QUASAR通过在训练循环内持续进行轻量级损失感知重建，有效弥合了QAT中更新目标与代理目标之间的失配，降低了损失下限，在多个语言模型和比特率设置下均优于现有QAT和PTQ基线，且不引入推理开销。

Abstract: As large language model inference shifts toward lower precision, post-training quantization (PTQ) becomes increasingly brittle, making quantization-aware training (QAT) essential for preserving model quality. However, QAT computes the loss and surrogate gradients using a lossy reconstruction of latent full-precision weights, while applying updates to the latent weights themselves. This mismatch can lead to suboptimal training trajectories and a higher loss floor. Second-order PTQ methods mitigate a similar gap by minimizing loss-aware reconstruction error, but doing it once for a frozen model can take hours; repeating this process throughout QAT as the weights evolve is impractical. We introduce QUASAR, a QAT method that continuously performs lightweight, loss-aware reconstruction in the training loop to lower the loss floor and improve the resulting low-bit model. At each training step, QUASAR uses the exponential moving average of squared gradients as online saliency estimates, searches over a small set of clipping ranges, and fits affine dequantizers via saliency-weighted least squares. Our analysis shows that the loss-aware reconstruction error is the only reconstruction-dependent term in the QAT convergence bound and controls the loss of the final quantized model, establishing QUASAR's objective as a principled optimization target. QUASAR modifies only the training procedure and supports standard deployment formats, including integer quantization and NVFP4, with no inference-time changes or overhead. Across Qwen3 and Llama-3.1, QUASAR achieves the lowest held-out KL divergence among competitive QAT methods at 2, 3, and 4 bits, reducing KL by at least 10% at 3 and 4 bits and by 29% at 2 bits. At 2 bits, it improves average accuracy across eight tasks by 3.5-4.3 percentage points over strong QAT and PTQ baselines.

</details>


### [76] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一种两阶段的对撞机物理事件级异常检测框架，先通过监督对比学习在多样的物理过程上学习嵌入空间，再在该空间中运行标准自编码器生成异常分数。在高亮度大型强子对撞机模拟数据上，ORCA在灵敏度广度和深度上均优于基线自编码器，且对比嵌入使异常样本具有可解释性——通过最大似然模板拟合可对异常事件进行归因并量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机物理中通用事件级异常检测存在两个反复出现的问题：异常分数难以解释，且分数与能量尺度及物体多重性高度相关。需要一种既能提升检测灵敏度又能提供可解释性的方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过监督对比学习在多样物理过程集合上学习嵌入空间；第二阶段在该空间运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。此外，利用嵌入空间中已知过程占据不同区域的特点，通过最大似然模板拟合对异常事件进行归因分析。

Result: 在高亮度LHC模拟数据上，ORCA相比基线自编码器在新物理信号检测的广度和深度上均有显著提升。模板拟合能准确恢复注入信号产额（包括训练中未见的信号），并能对模板库中不存在的信号通过最相似已知过程进行特征化。

Conclusion: ORCA为对撞机上基于可解释异常检测的搜索提供了可行路径。嵌入几何携带比标准一维输出拟合更高维的物理信息，增强了下游统计分析能力。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [77] [When Does More Correct Data Hurt? Insertion-Stability and the Limits of Dimension-Based Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14020)
*Joseph Sankoorikal Johny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"单调对抗者"（monotone adversary）在训练数据中插入正确标记样本时对学习算法的影响。作者定义"插入稳定"（insertion-stable）学习器：额外输入正确标记的样本只会缩小其出错区域，这类学习器对对抗者免疫。作者证明经典维度（VC维、Littlestone维）无法预测免疫性，并揭示在Mehrotra的困难类上，任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到干净速率，指出问题本质在于学习器与类别是否匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人用单调对抗者模拟了"添加正确数据反而有害"的失败模式，Mehrotra已证明对于VC维d>=2的类别，最坏情况下期望误差为Theta((d/n)log(en/d))，比干净PAC速率高出对数因子。但该速率只描述所有类别的最坏情况，无法回答哪些类别实际承受惩罚、哪些学习器能避免惩罚，这正是本文要解决的问题。

Method: 定义"插入稳定学习器"：馈入更多正确标记的样本只会缩小其错误区域。对于此类学习器，插入后的风险永远不会超过干净部分的风险，因此对单调对抗者免疫。作者进一步分析经典维度（VC维和Littlestone维）是否能预测免疫性，并通过构造压缩方案（compression scheme）的不可行性来证明免疫性的结构性条件。

Result: （1）插入稳定学习器对单调对抗者免疫，高概率保证直接保留；（2）Closure学习器是插入稳定的，因此所有交闭类保持 E[Err] <= (21d+34)/n 的干净速率；（3）VC维=Littlestone维=2的两个类别可分裂为Theta(1/n)与Theta(log(en)/n)两种速率，证明经典维度无法预测免疫性；（4）区间类的Littlestone维无界但免疫；（5）在Mehrotra的困难类上，任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到干净速率。

Conclusion: 问题不在于类别本身是否困难，也不在于学习器本身是否优秀，而在于两者是否匹配。若存在一个对干净数据最优的插入稳定学习器，则正确的添加是免费的；若不存在，则该代价归属于类别本身，更换学习器也无法避免。

Abstract: Adding data known to be correct ought to be safe. Not always. Larsen, Pabbaraju and Shetty model the failure with a monotone adversary, which reads an i.i.d. training sample and may append as many further examples as it likes, provided the target hypothesis labels them all. Mehrotra has since settled the cost, showing that for classes of VC dimension d >= 2 no learner can guarantee expected error better than Theta((d/n)log(en/d)), a logarithmic factor above the clean PAC rate.
  Because that rate is a worst case over all classes, it says nothing about which classes actually suffer the penalty, and the answer turns on the learner. We call a learner insertion-stable if feeding it more correctly labeled examples can only shrink the region where it errs. Such learners are immune to the adversary, since on any given sample the risk after insertions never exceeds the risk on the clean part alone, however much is added and however cleverly it is chosen. High- probability guarantees carry over unchanged, and because Closure is insertion-stable every intersection-closed class keeps its clean rate of E[Err] <= (21d+34)/n.
  Immunity is not something the classical dimensions can predict. Two classes can agree on VCdim = Ldim = 2 and still split, one at Theta(1/n) and the other at Theta(log(en)/n), while intervals have unbounded Littlestone dimension and are immune anyway. On Mehrotra's hard class we prove more than the failure of a single algorithm, showing that no monotone permutation-invariant compression scheme of any finite size attains the clean rate.
  The question is therefore not whether a class is hard, nor whether a learner is good, but whether the two suit each other. Given an insertion-stable learner that is optimal on clean data, correct additions are free, and without one the cost belongs to the class, so changing the learner will not avoid it.

</details>


### [78] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出查询派生擦除方向（QED），为线性注意力/Delta Rule模型增加一个与键正交、由查询派生的第二擦除方向，以提高长上下文下的检索能力，使可用上下文长度翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性注意力中，固定大小的状态在长上下文下产生项目间干扰，降低检索效果。现有Delta Rule模型（如GDN-2）的擦除向量均从当前token的键派生，但读操作中的干扰是通过查询测量的，擦除步骤无法触及查询方向上的干扰，导致检索质量受限。

Method: 提出QED（Query-derived Erase Direction）：在GDN-2原有从键派生的擦除方向基础上，增加一个由查询派生、与键正交的第二擦除方向。在快速权重视角下，键方向的delta编辑无法改变读操作中键正交部分的内容，QED利用可编辑部分来抵消沿查询方向测量的旧状态内容。

Result: QED在超过训练窗口的所有长度上均改善检索性能，在S-NIAH-1基准上将可用上下文长度大约翻倍。

Conclusion: 通过引入查询派生的正交擦除方向，解决了现有Delta Rule模型擦除方向与读方向不匹配的问题，显著提升线性注意力在长上下文下的检索能力。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [79] [Forecast Collapse in Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14106)
*Shu Wan,Miles Ma,Hank Zhu,Guangqi Liu,Stephen Wang,Qingsong Wen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在预测1000只美国股票的每小时收益时，发现预测变得近乎平坦且股票排序能力差（称为"预测坍缩"），但预测交易量时该现象大幅消失。研究发现这与目标可预测性密切相关，存在校准-排序权衡，并提出CalibRank目标函数解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列评估仅关注每个序列自身的校准误差（如MSE），忽略了跨序列结构（如股票间的横截面排序），这导致在预测股票收益时出现预测坍缩这一盲点，而下游决策（如量化投资）恰恰依赖于跨序列的相对排序。

Method: 首先系统性考察预测坍缩现象在TSFM、12种深度学习模型及97个公开基准配置中的表现，识别出两大成因：低可预测性限制校准点预测的幅度，以及单序列目标函数无法识别跨序列结构。随后提出CalibRank目标函数，在平方误差（校准）与横截面相关性（排序）之间取得平衡。

Result: 在Finance1K基准上，CalibRank将近乎三倍提升横截面相关性，同时保持预测幅度接近真实目标值，并在所有测试模型上均改善了相关性。

Conclusion: 研究发现揭示了传统时间序列评估的盲区：逐序列指标可能掩盖下游决策所需跨序列结构的失败。校准-排序权衡是预测坍缩的本质，CalibRank提供了一个简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: When forecasting hourly returns for 1,000 US equities, we observe an unexpected phenomenon: predictions become nearly flat and show poor stock ranking, as measured by cross-sectional correlation. We call this forecast collapse. Surprisingly, the phenomenon largely disappears when forecasting trading volume under the same setting. We investigate forecast collapse across time-series foundation models (TSFMs), twelve deep-learning forecasting models, and 97 public benchmark configurations, and find that it is closely tied to target predictability. We identify two distinct reasons behind it: low predictability limits the amplitude of calibrated point forecasts, while per-series objectives leave cross-series structure unidentified. These findings reveal a calibration-ranking tradeoff: optimizing squared error leads to flat predictions, whereas directly optimizing cross-sectional correlation improves ranking but can inflate forecast amplitude by more than an order of magnitude. To address this tradeoff, we introduce CalibRank, a simple objective that balances calibration and ranking. On Finance1K, CalibRank nearly triples cross-sectional correlation while keeping amplitude close to the target, and improves correlation on all tested models. Our results reveal a blind spot in conventional time-series evaluation: per-series metrics can hide failures in cross-series structure needed by downstream decisions.

</details>


### [80] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2018年10月至2026年7月间AI模型从BERT等简单系统演进为能解复杂数学题并编写软件的大型智能体的过程。真实编码问题解决能力自2024年底以来每年提升近6倍，同时成本大幅下降。顶级性能目前分散在多个专长模型中，不同模型在各自领域领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪和评估AI模型在编码、数学推理等领域的快速进步，并展示性能提升与成本下降并行的趋势，同时验证推理方法（如高级采样、置信度排序工具）在提升模型能力方面的有效性与实用性。

Method: 通过多维度对比分析：1) 评估真实编码问题解决能力的年度进步率；2) 对比不同模型的性能与成本（如GPT-5.6 Luna以低价达到旗舰水平）；3) 按应用领域（前端编码、仓库级编码、终端任务）对顶级模型进行分类；4) 在小学数学测试中对比基础方法与高级采样方法（Qwen-2.5模型，58/100 vs 79/100）；5) 使用置信度排序工具评估其识别正确答案的能力（前50次选择中识别出47个正确）。

Result: 自2024年底以来真实编码问题解决能力每年提升近6倍；GPT-5.6 Luna以每百万token仅1-6美元的成本媲美旗舰能力；模型呈现专长分化趋势（Claude Opus 5领先前端编码，Claude Fable 5擅长仓库级编码，GPT-5.6 Sol主导终端任务）；高级采样在小学数学测试中将解题率从58%提升至79%；置信度排序工具在前50次选择中正确识别47个正确答案。

Conclusion: AI模型在编码和数学推理方面取得了显著进步，性能与成本效益同时改善。不同模型在不同领域各具优势，推理策略（高级采样、置信度排序）能有效提升实际任务表现。所有研究材料已完全公开，便于复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [81] [Revisiting Energy-based Tabular Anomaly Detection: Energy and Reconstruction are Complementary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14186)
*Junichiro Niimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视经典深度玻尔兹曼机（DBM）用于表格异常检测。在两个表格基准（UCI Bank Marketing和NSL-KDD）上，DBM的均场能量在Bank Marketing上与最强基线自编码器持平，在NSL-KDD上显著胜出，并显著优于其余七个基线。将DBM能量与自编码器通过秩融合结合后，在两个数据集上均获得统计显著的AUROC提升，而所有非DBM基线的融合均未改善或显著降低性能。作者主张经典EBM应作为与重建类方法互补的视角纳入表格异常检测工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格异常检测方法（密度代理、重建类、非参数评分器）都仅间接逼近内在分布，而显式能量模型（EBM）在表格异常检测中基本缺失。作者借深度学习领域EBM复兴之势，假设DBM的均场能量能与重建类评分形成比同源方法更好的互补组合。

Method: 使用两层隐藏层的DBM，其均场自由能作为异常评分；在UCI Bank Marketing和NSL-KDD两个数据集上，与八个经典的现代基线（Isolation Forest、OCSVM、LOF、Autoencoder、VAE、COPOD、ECOD、Deep SVDD）进行20个随机种子的对比；随后将DBM能量与自编码器通过秩融合结合，验证其对集成性能的增量贡献。

Result: DBM均场能量在Bank Marketing上与Autoencoder并列最佳，在NSL-KDD上统计显著优于Autoencoder，并在两个数据集上均显著优于其余七个基线。秩融合后，DBM+AE在Bank Marketing上AUROC提升+0.014（p<0.01），在NSL-KDD上+0.002（p<0.001），均统计显著；而非DBM衍生的基线与AE融合则无改善或显著降级。

Conclusion: 经典EBM（以DBM为代表）应当在表格异常检测工具包中占有一席之地，作为与当前主导的重建类评分互补的非冗余视角，能有效提升集成检测性能。

Abstract: Tabular anomaly detection is dominated by classical density-proxy methods (Isolation Forest, OCSVM, LOF), reconstruction-based detectors (Autoencoders, VAEs), and modern non-parametric scorers (COPOD, ECOD, Deep SVDD), all of which approximate the inlier distribution only indirectly; explicit energy-based models are largely absent. Motivated by the recent revival of EBMs in deep learning (e.g., Energy-Based Transformers, JEPA), we revisit the classical Deep Boltzmann Machine (DBM) for this task and hypothesize that its mean-field energy combines more effectively with a reconstruction-based score than same-lineage pairs do. We evaluate a two-hidden-layer DBM on two tabular benchmarks spanning distinct domains (UCI Bank Marketing and NSL-KDD) against eight classical and modern baselines across twenty random seeds. The DBM mean-field energy matches the strongest baseline (the Autoencoder) on Bank Marketing and statistically beats it on NSL-KDD, while significantly outperforming the remaining seven on both datasets. When fused with the Autoencoder via rank fusion, the DBM energy yields a statistically significant improvement on both datasets (AUROC=+0.014, p<0.01 on Bank Marketing; +0.002, p<0.001 on NSL-KDD); every non-DBM-derived base model instead fails to improve or significantly degrades the AE-paired ensemble. Our position is that classical EBMs, exemplified by the DBM, deserve a place in the tabular anomaly detection toolbox as a non-redundant complementary view to the reconstruction-based scores that dominate current practice.

</details>


### [82] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*
