Through comprehensive analyses of representations, alignment strategies, training data, and history length, we establish that compact transaction embeddings are a superior approach to transaction representation than text serialization for multimodal reasoning and zero-shot prediction tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209)
*Hengzhe Zhang,Qi Chen,Bing Xue,Lean Yu,Wolfgang Banzhaf,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性指标在演化过程中选择性保留已构建的特征，避免有价值基因材料丢失，从而提升符号回归及其它领域的搜索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演化特征构建方法在符号回归中表现良好，但缺乏显式机制来保留演化过程中发现的重要构建特征。当遗传算子破坏有效特征时，有价值的遗传材料会丢失，影响解的质量。

Method: 提出一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性度量来选择性保留演化过程中构建的特征。该机制对更重要的构建特征提供更强的保护，同时允许较不重要的特征被修改，并能从更重要特征中吸收有用的构造块。采用多种特征重要性计算方法进行评估，并测试不同基础学习器下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在98个回归基准数据集上的实验表明，所提机制在解质量上持续优于基线方法；在两个信用分类数据集上的实验进一步证明该方法可有效扩展到符号回归之外的搜索任务中。

Conclusion: 基于特征重要性的自适应保护机制能有效防止有价值的特征遗传材料丢失，一致提升解质量，且方法具有跨任务领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Evolutionary feature construction has shown strong promise in symbolic regression by automatically discovering informative transformations of input features that enhance a simple base learner. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to preserve important constructed features discovered during evolution, and valuable genetic material can be lost when genetic operators disrupt effective features. This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. We evaluate the approach using multiple feature importance calculation methods and demonstrate its robustness across different base learners. Experimental results on 98 regression benchmark datasets show that the proposed mechanism consistently improves solution quality over baseline approaches, and experiments on two credit classification datasets demonstrate that the method also extends effectively to improve search effectiveness beyond symbolic regression.

</details>


### [118] [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211)
*Patrik Kenfack,Jesse C. Cresswell,Anthony L. Caterini,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairTFM，一种将公平性约束直接集成到表格基础模型（TFM）训练中的方法，通过合成公平性任务和基于梯度反转层的公平性感知架构，使模型在单次前向传播中即可实现公平预测，在132个公平性任务上一致提升了公平性同时保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）已广泛应用于高风险决策场景，但其公平性属性尚未被充分探索。现有公平性技术无法直接应用于上下文学习范式，且训练数据中敏感属性访问受限，这两个关键挑战促使作者提出将公平性约束直接纳入TFM训练过程的方法。

Method: 提出FairTFM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于合成公平性任务的可扩展训练策略，应对训练数据中敏感属性访问受限的问题；(2) 采用梯度反转层（Gradient Reversal Layer）的公平性感知架构，鼓励模型学习与敏感属性不变的表示，从而在单次前向传播中实现公平预测。

Result: 在132个公平性任务上的实验表明，FairTFM能够一致性地提升模型公平性，同时保持具有竞争力的预测精度。

Conclusion: FairTFM通过将公平性约束直接嵌入TFM训练，解决了公平性技术与上下文学习范式不兼容的问题，为在高风险场景中部署公平的表格基础模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as leading methods for tabular predictive tasks, leveraging in-context learning to predict on new data without task-specific training. Despite the increased use of TFMs in high-stakes decision-making, their fairness properties remain largely unexplored. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass. Our approach addresses two key challenges: limited access to sensitive attributes in training data, and the incompatibility of existing fairness techniques with the in-context learning paradigm. We propose FairTFM, a scalable training strategy based on synthetic fairness tasks and a fairness-aware architecture using a gradient reversal layer, which encourages the model to learn representations invariant to sensitive attributes. Experiments on 132 fairness tasks show consistent improvements in fairness while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215)
*Johanna Hillebrand,Jan Höckendorff,Jürgen Kusche,Kelin Luo,Heiko Röglin,Melanie Schmidt,Christian Sohler,Bernd Uebbing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连通子空间聚类（Connected Subspace Clustering）问题，用于在高维数据中寻找既内部相似又物理连通的聚类。作者证明了该问题即使在简单情形下也难以近似，并提出了一种基于Lloyd算法的启发式方法，交替进行子空间拟合和迭代合并以强制连通性。方法在大地测量学（海平面时间序列）应用中显著优于无约束方法和其他策略，能够识别出与气候指数对齐的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大地测量学等应用中，需要对不同物理位置的测量数据进行分组，要求聚类既在特征空间内部相似又在物理空间上连通。现有无约束聚类方法会产生大量不连通的碎片（如研究中多达1,966个），无法满足实际需求，因此需要引入约束条件建模物理连通性。

Method: 提出Connected Subspace Clustering问题：给定高维点和连通图，将点划分为k个连通簇，最小化各簇到其最优m'维仿射子空间的总平方距离。理论方面证明该问题在m'=0且网格图带洞时即为NP-hard，且难以在Ω(n^{1/2-ε})因子内近似。算法方面提出Lloyd风格的启发式：交替进行子空间拟合，并采用迭代合并过程来强制连通性，确保返回恰好k个连通区域。

Result: 方法在global sea level时间序列上进行了160种配置的评估：在73.75%的情况下基于合并的修复策略是最强的；始终优于（连通）Ward's方法等竞争者；无约束方法产生多达1,966个不连通碎片而本文方法由构造保证恰好k个连通区域。识别出的区域与El Nino-Southern Oscillation和Indian Ocean Dipole等气候指数对齐。

Conclusion: 虽然方法源于大地测量学，但可推广到其他空间嵌入的多变量时间序列领域，如气候场、遥感、神经影像和传感器网络。

Abstract: Constrained optimization extends classical optimization by integrating side information, making it widely applicable across scientific and engineering domains. Consider a setting where we measure variables at different physical locations. When grouping these measurements, we often want clusters that are both internally similar and physically coherent. Thus, we have a constrained clustering problem where the constraint models coherence. Motivated by an application in geodesy, where contiguous regions of the sea surface must be identified for principal component analysis, we introduce the Connected Subspace Clustering problem: given high-dimensional points and a connectivity graph, partition them into $k$ connected clusters, minimizing their total squared distance to the clusters' best-fit $m'$-dimensional affine subspaces. We prove that, even for $m' = 0$ and a grid graph with holes, the problem is NP-hard to approximate within $Ω(n^{1/2-\varepsilon})$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ is the number of measurements. We then introduce an efficient Lloyd-style heuristic that alternates subspace fitting with an iterative merging procedure to enforce connectivity. Our method returns exactly $k$ connected regions by construction, whereas unconstrained methods leave up to $1{,}966$ disconnected fragments at higher cost. In a study of 160 configurations on global sea level time series, our merging-based repair is the strongest of four strategies in $73.75\%$ of cases, and consistently outperforms competitors such as (connected) Ward's method across all tested cluster counts. The resulting regions isolate signals aligning with climate indices such as the El Nino-Southern Oscillation and Indian Ocean Dipole. Although developed for geodesy, the approach applies to other spatially embedded multivariate time series, such as climate fields, remote sensing, neuroimaging, and sensor networks.

</details>


### [120] [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSchema，一个无需训练的通用的实时模式接地（live schema grounding）框架，使语言模型代理能够直接从当前SPARQL端点获取查询所需的模式证据，替代手工维护的元数据文件，在生物医学知识图谱问答等多个任务上优于TogoMCP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学知识图谱通过SPARQL提供丰富的结构化数据，但不同资源使用各自的模式（schema）、标识符和链接。TogoMCP通过人工维护的Metadata Interoperability Exchange文件帮助语言模型代理查询这些资源，但创建和维护这些文件仍然需要语言模型辅助草拟、验证和人工审查，成本高昂且难以扩展到新资源。因此需要一种无需预先准备模式文件的自动化方法。

Method: AutoSchema是一个无需训练的实时模式接地通用框架，包含四个核心步骤：1) 检查实时模式（inspect live schemas）；2) 将问题中的实体名称映射到图谱标识符；3) 探索关系路径（relation paths）；4) 在迭代查询构建过程中发现不同资源间的可能连接。该框架直接在查询时从当前端点获取模式证据，不需要预先创建和维护模式文件。

Result: 在Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA、Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA、Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA（BioASQ Task B）以及Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer（迁移到此前未记录的RDF图）四个任务上评估。AutoSchema在生物医学KGQA任务上相比TogoMCP提升了平均事实准确性，在纵向BioASQ评估中持续获得增益；同时减少了迭代预算耗尽的情况，在核心评估中平均使用更少的工具调用。迁移实验初步表明实时模式接地可以支持不规则的、未见过的新图而无需预先创建模式文件。

Conclusion: 实时模式接地（live schema grounding）是一种可行且有效的替代方案，能够消除对人工维护模式文件的依赖，使语言模型代理能够动态适应不同知识图谱资源，同时提升查询准确性和效率。AutoSchema为知识图谱问答提供了一个更具扩展性的一般性框架，其迁移能力暗示该方法可推广到更广泛的领域。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [121] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOBO-Merge框架，将模型合并参数选择建模为黑盒多目标优化问题，利用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下近似Pareto前沿。在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上的两模型和三模型合并实验中，MOBO-Merge在12组对比的11组中获得了比随机搜索更高的平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并（model merging）直接在权重空间中组合已训练模型，是比微调更省计算成本的替代方案。然而，选择合并参数（merge parameters）非常困难，原因在于：(1)下游评估代价高昂；(2)无法获得梯度；(3)不同来源模型的能力可能相互冲突。这些问题使得高效、系统地搜索合并参数成为一个关键挑战。

Method: 本文提出MOBO-Merge，一个与具体合并算子无关（merge-operator agnostic）的框架，将合并参数选择形式化为黑盒多目标优化问题，使用多目标贝叶斯优化（multi-objective Bayesian optimization）在有限的评估预算下逼近Pareto前沿。实验中使用了Linear、SLERP、TIES和block-wise等四种合并算子，并设置了Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B的两模型（指令-数学）和三模型（指令-数学-代码）合并场景。

Result: 在保留的基准测试分区上，MOBO-Merge在12组对比中有11组获得了比随机搜索更高的平均超体积（hypervolume）。在一维Linear插值场景下增益较小，但在TIES、block-wise以及三目标搜索场景下增益显著。此外，没有单一合并算子始终最优：TIES在四个family-setting组合中有三个表现最佳，而Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达能力强的合并参数化的搜索层具有显著价值。MOBO-Merge为模型合并中昂贵且困难的黑盒参数选择问题提供了一种有效的解决方案，且不依赖于特定的合并算子。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [122] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多种新的凸损失函数用于SVM和神经网络的二分类任务，理论上可泛化标准损失，并通过粒子群优化求解原始SVM问题，实验表明带/不带新损失的泛化性能无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在损失函数中引入模式相关性（pattern correlations）是否能提升分类模型的泛化性能，弥补现有标准损失函数在利用数据内在结构方面的不足。

Method: 提出多种新的凸损失函数，推广标准损失；由于对偶SVM难以利用这些损失，采用SVM原始形式并结合粒子群优化算法求解；通过嵌套交叉验证评估各损失的性能。

Result: 在多个小型数据集上实验，结果表明使用新损失函数与标准损失函数的泛化指标相同，未观察到显著性能提升。但该初步研究暗示在理论上将模式相关性引入损失函数可能增强某些数据集的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 新提出的凸损失函数虽在理论上可行且能泛化标准损失，但在当前实验设置下未能带来实际的泛化性能提升，属于初步探索性研究。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [123] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CodeSIFT，一种不依赖威胁模型预设的检测方法，利用影响函数识别批量诱导LLM生成不安全代码的恶意提示。在三个开放权重代码LLM（3B-7B）上评估，AUROC最高达0.98，优于静态分析基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM代码生成的防御方法依赖于预定义的威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，无法有效应对新型攻击，需要一种无需先验攻击知识的威胁模型无关检测方法。

Method: CodeSIFT通过影响函数（influence functions）测量生成代码在参数空间中的影响力，利用统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布，从而识别异常模型行为，而非检测特定漏洞类型。同时引入了两个覆盖多种漏洞类型的基准数据集用于评估。

Result: 在三个开放权重代码LLM（3B至7B参数）上，CodeSIFT在中高注入率下AUROC达0.98，误报率校准良好，且显著优于静态分析基线。

Conclusion: 基于影响函数的检测为识别恶意代码生成提示提供了有前景的方向，无需预先了解攻击类别即可有效检测，扩展了LLM代码生成安全防御的能力边界。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子多臂老虎机（QMAB）和量子线性老虎机（QLB）的后悔界问题，首次证明了 QMAB 的极小极大下界 Ω(K log(T/K)) 和有限动作 QLB 的 Ω(d log(T/d))，并提出了一个基于设计的消除算法，将有限动作 QLB 的后悔从先前的 d² 依赖改进为线性 d 依赖（匹配下界至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作给出了 QMAB 的 O(K log T) 和 QLB 的 O(d² polylog T) 后悔上界，但未回答 K log T 尺度是否不可避免以及 d² 依赖能否改进的问题。Wan et al. [2023] 明确提出了是否可实现与 T 无关的后悔这一开放问题，本文旨在通过极小极大下界和更优的算法来解答。

Method: 本文的核心方法包括：(1) 通过多项式方法和三角函数多项式上的 Remez 型不等式，证明高置信度单臂量子检验下界（区分固定奖励均值与区间内替代值）；(2) 通过 bandit-to-testing 归约将其提升为 QMAB 下界，通过线性嵌入得到有限动作 QLB 下界；(3) 提出基于设计的消除算法，将低偏置低方差量子均值估计器与小支持的 G-最优设计相结合，通过匹配设计权重的查询分配，使重建误差以方差而非最坏情形绝对误差的方式聚合。

Result: 证明了 QMAB 的极小极大后悔下界为 Ω(K log(T/K))，有限动作 QLB 的极小极大后悔下界为 Ω(d log(T/d))。对于动作集大小为 poly(d) 的有限动作 QLB，所提出的设计消除算法实现线性 d 后悔，改进先前的 d² 依赖并匹配下界（至多对数因子）。当使用量子蒙特卡洛估计时，维度依赖从 d² 降至 d^{3/2}，低方差估计器进一步去除剩余的 √d 因子。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 Wan et al. [2023] 提出的关于量子老虎机后悔是否可独立于 T 的开放问题，确立了 QMAB 和有限动作 QLB 在量子奖励预言机及其逆预言机模型下的极小极大后悔尺度，并提供了匹配至多对数因子的算法，在理论上闭合了量子线性老虎机的维度依赖差距。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [125] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的多模态轨迹预测方法，通过构建历史状态转移表和基于空间邻近性、航向、速度及时间上下文的乘积核进行邻居检索，在无需GPU和任何学习参数的情况下，达到了与5700万参数Transformer相当甚至更优的精度，尤其在数据稀缺场景下表现显著优于Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的轨迹预测方法（如Transformer）需要大量训练数据和GPU资源，在数据稀缺场景下性能急剧下降，且难以快速部署到新的地理区域。作者旨在开发一种无需训练、零学习参数的替代方案，既能保持预测精度又能克服数据稀缺问题。

Method: 该方法构建历史状态到下一位置对的转移表，并使用涵盖空间邻近性、航向（bearing）、速度和时间上下文的乘积核函数来检索最近邻。提供两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样（生成覆盖不同合理路线的轨迹）和束搜索（寻找最高似然路径），两者共享同一表征架构。

Result: 在TrAISformer基准（丹麦海事AIS数据）上，该方法在数据完整时达到与Transformer竞争的精度；在仅10%训练数据下仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer性能灾难性退化。该方法可利用少一个数量级的历史数据部署到新区域，且无需GPU训练。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了传统检索/表格方法在轨迹预测任务中仍具竞争力，尤其在数据稀缺和计算资源受限的实际部署场景下具有明显优势，为无需训练的轻量级轨迹预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [126] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对业务流程中延迟案例检测的难度进行了系统分析。通过在14个事件日志上的实验，发现剩余时间分布通常严重右偏，现有模型虽能较好捕捉分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现不佳；同时发现预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而显著增加（异方差性）。缓解类别不平衡的方法效果有限，表明核心问题可能不是不平衡而是与延迟案例相关的更高不确定性，而利用这种相关性可以显著改善延迟案例的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管剩余时间预测在深度学习架构方面已取得显著进展，但业界对延迟检测任务的固有难度缺乏理解。现有研究通常使用聚合指标评估模型性能，无法揭示模型在目标分布（尤其是运营上最关键的、具有大延迟的案例）上的表现差异，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 在14个事件日志上对延迟检测难度进行实证分析：首先检验剩余时间分布形态（右偏特性）；其次评估现有预测模型在分布不同区域（尤其是高延迟案例）的性能；然后分析预测不确定性与延迟幅度的关系（异方差性检验）；最后评估缓解类别不平衡的方法，并探索利用不确定性-延迟相关性改进延迟案例识别的策略。

Result: 研究发现：剩余时间通常呈强右偏分布，仅少数案例出现大延迟；现有模型能很好地捕捉分布众数，但在高延迟案例上表现差；存在显著的异方差性，即预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而增加；缓解不平衡问题的方法收益有限，表明根本问题可能不在于类别不平衡，而在于延迟案例固有的更高预测不确定性；利用该相关性可显著提升延迟案例的识别能力。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了延迟检测困难的根本来源——不是类别不平衡，而是延迟案例固有的更高预测不确定性。不确定性感知建模被确定为未来预测性流程监控研究中有前景的方向，为改进延迟检测提供了新的理论视角和方法路径。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [127] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为imposter的判别式自监督预训练任务，通过替换实体子集特征并要求编码器识别被替换的特征来学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。在ERA5-Land全球再分析数据（21个环境变量）上评估，并在7个下游任务（气候分类、碳通量估计、水流预测）上进行测试。研究发现没有单一目标在所有任务上占优，imposter在与现有SSL目标结合时能提供互补信息，表明物理一致性是科学基础模型有价值的新自监督来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据中的实体特征通常受物理定律共同约束，但现有的自监督学习目标大多忽略了这种物理一致性。作者希望设计一种能够利用数据内在物理依赖关系的预训练任务，从而让学习到的表征更好地反映物理规律。

Method: 提出了imposter这一判别式预训练任务：将实体的部分特征子集替换为另一实体的真实观测值，然后训练编码器识别哪些特征被替换了。由于每个替换值在个体层面上都是合理/真实的，模型必须学习跨特征的物理依赖关系才能正确识别。在ERA5-Land数据上使用21个环境变量进行预训练，并与其他SSL目标进行系统对比。

Result: 在7个下游任务上的评估表明：(1)最有效的预训练任务取决于下游任务类型，不存在单一目标普遍最优；(2)imposter与其他现有SSL目标结合时提供互补信息。这也是首次在共享架构和预训练预算下对地表建模的SSL目标进行系统比较。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型自监督学习中一个新的有价值信号来源。imposter任务能有效促使模型学习跨特征物理依赖，为地表建模等科学领域的自监督预训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [128] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机权重平均（SWA）作为一种无需重复训练多次的集成技术，用于将学习任务的对称性（通过数据增强）纳入神经网络。作者用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上提供的等变性提升超出了SWA单独带来的性能增益，并通过大量实验验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是将对称性纳入神经网络的有效方法，但无限深度集成的完美对称性需要重复训练多次，成本高昂。作者希望寻找一种无需重复训练即可获得类似对称性提升的替代方案。

Method: 用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练末期随机训练轨迹，在无限宽度极限下理论分析SWA在增强数据上的等变性；并在计算机视觉和图分类任务上（涵盖离散和连续对称性）进行数值实验验证。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，SWA结合数据增强能获得超越SWA单独性能提升所应带来的等变性增益；大量数值实验在多种模型和不同对称类型上验证了这一结论。

Conclusion: SWA是在无需重复训练的前提下，利用数据增强将对称性高质量地纳入深度学习模型的有效方法，其等变性提升在理论上可被精确刻画。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [129] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeaMoE是一种解码高效的MoE架构，通过将专家分组为"部门"并让同部门的专家共享大部分参数（仅保留少量私有参数），配合定制化的两阶段路由策略，在不损失模型精度和增加额外开销的情况下，有效缓解了小批量解码场景下MoE推理因专家权重加载导致的显存带宽瓶颈，最多可减少50.9%的每步加载权重，并实现最高2.00倍的解码速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在实时交互应用（如编码助手、实时音视频交互）中被广泛采用，但小批量解码场景下推理受限于专家权重加载的显存带宽瓶颈。现有解决方案（如训练后权重量化压缩、预训练时细粒度专家设计）要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销，该问题尚未得到充分关注。

Method: 提出DeaMoE架构：(1) 将专家按专业领域划分为若干"部门"，同一部门的专家共享大部分参数以反映其共同专业背景，同时每个专家保留少量私有参数以体现独特性；(2) 设计定制化的两阶段路由策略，避免冗余的权重加载，从而在大语言模型解码过程中显著提升效率。

Result: 与vanilla MoE相比，DeaMoE在预训练的7B模型上（A40）每步加载权重最多减少50.9%，端到端TPOT加速最高达1.33倍；在DeepSeek-V3的微基准测试中，A40和H40上的峰值加速分别达到2.00倍和1.97倍。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过参数共享的部门式专家分组和两阶段路由策略，有效解决了小批量解码下MoE模型的显存带宽瓶颈问题，在不牺牲模型精度的前提下显著提升了解码效率，为实时低延迟AI应用提供了一个可行的MoE架构方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [130] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CytoBERT，一个面向单细胞流式细胞术数据、支持可变标记物面板的开源基础模型。该模型在包含15个人类数据集、超过5000万个细胞的大规模语料上通过自监督预训练，学习可迁移的标记物间关系，并展示了跨异质性数据集进行迁移学习的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式细胞术数据因实验方案和测量特征选择不同而高度异质且缺乏标准化，导致机器学习方法难以跨研究迁移。尽管基础模型在其他领域取得进展，但在流式细胞术领域仍缺乏相应的基础模型。

Method: CytoBERT采用自监督预训练方式，在一个包含15个人类数据集、超过5000万个细胞、具有异质性标记物面板的大规模细胞术语料上进行训练。通过标记物标准化处理使不同面板的数据可对齐，模型学习细胞内部可迁移的标记物间关系。随后通过微调进行样本级分类任务。

Result: 在样本级分类任务的微调中，CytoBERT证明跨异质性流式细胞术数据集的迁移学习是可行的，展示了基础模型在该领域的应用潜力。

Conclusion: CytoBERT作为公开可用的基础模型，为可扩展、可泛化的流式细胞术分析提供了一个起点，有望推动该领域大规模、标准化分析的进展。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [131] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Power Sampling在推理时的一个悖论：它虽然能提高正确轨迹的概率质量，却反而降低了下游推理（如self-consistency）的性能（准确率最多下降18.5个百分点）。作者将原因归咎于剂量失配与覆盖失配，并提出一种变形控制、保留支持的Power目标来修复该问题，从而在相同预算下超越标准的多采样推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling作为一种无需验证器的推理增强方法，有潜力作为通用前端服务于多种下游采样方法。但作者发现其存在反常的悖论：虽然能提高正确轨迹的概率质量，却反而损害了本应增强的下游推理性能。理解并解决这一悖论对于改进推理时采样方法至关重要。

Method: 作者先以self-consistency为代表案例，实证观察到Power Sampling导致准确率下降最多18.5个百分点。然后通过诊断将其归因于两个失配：1）剂量失配——固定的指数在不同问题上导致差异巨大的分布变化程度；2）覆盖失配——全局锐化将质量集中在少数主导路径上，高pass@k并不代表保留了广泛的推理路径支持。基于此，作者提出用变形控制、保留支持的Power目标替代统一的轨迹指数化，对不同问题校准锐化强度，同时限制对中等概率路径的过度抑制。

Result: 在相同预算下（与加权self-consistency结合），修复后的采样器逆转了全局Power造成的性能损失，并在多个推理基准上超越了标准多采样推理方法。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的锐化效应需要精细控制：仅仅把概率质量推向正确轨迹并不够，还必须保留下游推理所需的分布式推理路径支持。通过变形控制和支持保留机制，可以在保持锐化收益的同时避免破坏下游聚合与搜索的有效性。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [132] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用的双层优化NAS框架，通过将离散的神经元和激活层决策替换为连续松弛，实现了三种可扩展的NAS方法（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA），在MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多样架构上识别紧凑且高性能的架构，实验结果验证了其在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS天然是双层优化问题，但由于离散架构决策、指数级搜索空间以及候选架构训练的高成本，计算开销巨大。现有方法难以在效率和效果之间取得良好平衡，需要一种可扩展且通用的框架来同时优化架构和参数。

Method: 提出三种可扩展的连续松弛公式化方法：(1) 基于神经元门控的NAS（NAS-NG），对神经元进行连续门控；(2) 基于混合激活的NAS（NAS-MA），对激活函数进行连续混合；(3) 结合两者的NAS-NGMA。通过将离散决策替换为连续松弛，使原本组合式的架构空间可进行可微优化，在双层优化框架中同时优化架构和网络参数。

Result: 在MNIST上，NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试准确率；NAS-NG仅以0.26M CNN参数就达到99.63%准确率。在CIFAR-10上，所提方法持续优于vanilla DARTS。此外，NAS-NG能优化过度参数化和文献最优的架构，在减少参数的同时提升准确率。

Conclusion: 松弛化双层优化可作为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，提供了一个通用的神经元与激活级别架构优化框架，能有效识别紧凑且高性能的架构，适用于多种网络类型。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [133] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的可微神经架构搜索框架，利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建线性规划来计算架构更新方向，在保持模型参数最优性的同时提升泛化能力。在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，LP-DARTS在收敛速度和验证性能上均优于标准DARTS及其变体，并在ImageNet上验证了架构的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微NAS通过将离散架构搜索空间松弛为连续域，使得连续优化原理可以应用于NAS。然而，现有的可微NAS方法在架构优化过程中未能充分利用训练和验证损失之间的数学关系来同时保证泛化性能和参数最优性，导致收敛速度慢、搜索效率不高。

Method: LP-NAS将可微NAS架构搜索问题形式化为一个线性规划问题：利用验证损失梯度构造目标函数（提升泛化），利用训练损失Hessian构造约束条件（保持模型参数最优性），通过求解LP获得架构更新方向。论文提出两种计算高效的变体：S-LP-NAS（简化版）和R-LP-NAS（正则化版），并将其应用于DARTS搜索空间得到S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS两个算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的大量实验表明，LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS，且在搜索早期迭代中就实现了更快的收敛和更高的验证性能。与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS和STO-DARTS等DARTS变体对比，也展现出有效性。在ImageNet上的实验验证了搜索到架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LP-NAS通过将可微NAS与数学规划（线性规划）相结合，提供了一种高效且可泛化到广泛连续搜索空间的NAS框架，证明了利用优化理论工具可以显著提升可微NAS的搜索效率和架构质量。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [134] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sMuon优化器，通过在低秩参数化下线性化并利用最小二乘法近似求解松弛的Muon目标函数，解决了Muon优化器无法与LoRA自然结合的问题，并在SFT和ReLoRA预训练实验中取得适度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中表现优于其他优化器，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中应用较少。主要原因是LoRA的低秩参数化在数学上无法对权重更新进行正交化，导致Muon无法与LoRA自然结合。作者希望解决这一兼容性问题，使Muon优化器也能惠及低秩微调场景。

Method: 作者通过在线性化（linearization）后使用最小二乘法（least-squares）近似求解低秩设置下松弛的Muon目标函数。实现上仅使用矩阵乘法（matmul）操作，避免了更复杂的线性代数分解例程。

Result: sMuon在监督微调（SFT）和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好。尽管结果因模型和评测任务而异，但总体而言，使用Muon进行低秩微调提供了适度的性能改进。

Conclusion: sMuon成功地将Muon优化器的优势扩展到低秩参数高效微调场景，通过巧妙的数学近似和高效实现，在不需要复杂分解的情况下实现了与LoRA的兼容，为PEFT领域提供了新的优化器选择。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [135] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段的跨表格数据生成（CTDG）框架：第一阶段将异构原始表转换为统一的统计表，第二阶段使用扩散Transformer模型从多张异构表中学习生成合成统计表，再重建为原始格式的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成表格数据生成方法主要局限于单输入表场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格。跨表格数据生成需要从多个异构表中学习统一的生成模型，但现有方法缺乏此能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 第一阶段将每张异构原始表转换为标准化统计表，所有表拥有相同列集合，捕获原始列的边际分布和成对相关性；2) 第二阶段训练扩散Transformer模型学习同质统计表的结构模式并生成合成统计表；3) 通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换从生成的统计表重建合成原始表。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在学习的统计表示上具有高保真度，并在生成合成数据时实现了良好的保真度-多样性权衡，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架实现了从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型，并支持生成无限数量的真实感合成异构表格，填补了现有方法在跨表生成场景中的空白。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [136] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex是一个为视觉语言模型(VLM)强化学习(RL)后训练设计的运行时系统，通过将参考评分和训练阶段的prefix计算移到rollout解码窗口内执行，实现阶段级流水线重叠，在32块H800 GPU上取得1.57×–2.24×的加速比，同时保持同步RL更新语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy RL训练运行时对文本和VLM采用相同的串行阶段执行方式（rollout→reference scoring→actor training），但在VLM中处理密集视频输入和prompt前缀占据了大量计算，且prefix处理与生成响应无关，可以在rollout解码的同时执行，当前做法浪费了GPU计算资源。

Method: Rollplex将reference和training阶段进行分解，把prefix计算移入rollout解码窗口。技术上包括两个关键机制：(1) 相位感知的内存管理，根据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留；(2) 并行性感知的权重共享，对布局兼容的tensor在不同TP度数间使用同一物理存储，仅对不兼容的tensor进行重建，避免完整的第二份actor副本。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex相比串行colocation取得1.23×–1.30×加速比，相比相同GPU预算下的disaggregation方案取得1.57×–2.24×加速比，同时保持同步RL更新语义。

Conclusion: 通过利用VLM中prefix计算与响应生成的独立性，以及精心设计的显存管理和权重共享策略，Rollplex在不破坏同步on-policy RL语义的前提下显著提升了VLM RL后训练的吞吐效率，证明了阶段粒度执行对于VLM而言是过于保守的调度策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [137] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RecipeNet，一种分层Transformer架构，用于配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造）的表征学习。该架构通过堆叠的Transformer编码器分别建模步骤内部的字段级交互和跨步骤的顺序依赖，在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据在多个领域广泛存在，其结构为包含异质结构化字段的有序步骤序列。现有表格学习方法通常将这类结构展平为固定模式的表示，无法充分捕捉层次化的字段交互和过程依赖性。

Method: RecipeNet采用分层Transformer架构：底层Transformer编码器建模每个步骤内部的字段级交互，顶层Transformer编码器建模跨步骤的顺序依赖关系，通过堆叠的方式将两种层次的语义表征融合。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet持续优于现有表格学习模型，验证了分层和顺序建模对配方表征学习的价值。

Conclusion: 分层Transformer架构能够更有效地建模配方数据的层次结构和顺序依赖性，是配方表征学习的有效范式。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge通过反射性演化从少量带ground-truth标签的轨迹中自动生成可读的评判rubric，用冻结的7B模型同时作为智能体和评判者，在tau-bench和WebShop上相比G-Eval通用评判者，虽然总体一致性没有显著提升，但将误判失败轨迹（false-pass）的概率降低了约一半，更具忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模评估语言模型智能体时，自动评判器（LLM-as-judge）常被用作可执行环境奖励的替代品。然而现有方法（如G-Eval手工编写评分规则或微调评判器权重）倾向于给流畅但不成功的轨迹打高分，产生"false-pass"问题。缺乏一个无需环境访问、可信任且可归因的奖励无关评判器。

Method: RubricForge从少量有ground-truth标签的轨迹中，通过反射性演化（reflective evolution）生成评判rubric文本，最大化与环境奖励的一致性；然后冻结该rubric，在单次模型调用中应用于未见轨迹，全程无需环境访问。输出的rubric是人类可读文本，每个判定可归因到具名标准。

Result: 在tau-bench（173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上，使用同一个冻结7B模型作为智能体和评判者。与通用G-Eval评判者相比，总体一致性无显著差异（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略差（|err|差-0.048, p=2×10^-4）；但RubricForge的false-pass率约为G-Eval的一半（tau-bench上0.115 vs 0.173），WebShop排序更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370），且无逆转案例。

Conclusion: 对于奖励无关的评估器，部署相关的关键指标是false-pass率而非总体一致性——false-pass会交付有缺陷的智能体，而false-fail仅损失重试成本。RubricForge的增益在于忠实性（faithfulness）而非原始一致性。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [139] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行系统性逐层敏感性分析，发现深度依赖性至关重要：早期层（0-9）和中间层（10-29）对专家掩码高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39，尤其是35-39）能容忍激进的低幅度专家掩码。窄幅很深策略（35-39层@50%）在500提示验证集上保留419/500 Good+Similar输出，仅掩码640/10,240个专家，是质量/掩码专家数的最佳权衡。此外将top-k路由从8降至6在100提示探针上显著降低墙钟时间且无质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在提升LLM扩展性的同时通过稀疏激活保持计算效率，但单个MoE层的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，特别是在模型压缩场景下。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地理解不同深度MoE层对模型压缩的敏感性，为深度感知的专家掩码策略提供实证基础。

Method: 采用基于幅度的专家掩码方法，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上进行多阶段研究。实验跨越100、300、500个提示的评估规模，在三台H100 GPU服务器上执行。比较了全层统一掩码与针对不同层段的晚期聚焦策略，并额外评估了将top-k路由宽度从8降至6对墙钟时间的影响。

Result: 层敏感性呈现强深度依赖：早期层(0-9)和中间层(10-29)对专家掩码高度脆弱，晚期层(30-39)尤其是最晚期层(35-39)可容忍激进掩码。全层30%掩码在300提示下仅保留150/300良好输出，而晚期聚焦策略掩码640-1,145个专家时保留249-255/300。500提示验证集上，窄幅很深策略(35-39层@50%)保留419/500且仅掩码640/10,240专家。top-k从8降至6在100提示探针上大幅降低墙钟时间且无质量损失，但与激进专家掩码组合尚不理想。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了深度感知MoE专家掩码的经验基础，晚期层对压缩更鲁棒，为后续物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及基于训练的恢复等方向提供了实际路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [140] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析检验了大型语言模型（LLMs）是否像人脑一样发展出功能模块化组织结构。在覆盖四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的46个任务中，发现LLMs发展出了与人脑镜像对应的模块化架构：人脑中使用相同网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而使用不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元。这暗示模块化可能是有智能系统的基本属性而非生物大脑特有的进化偶然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑表现出显著的功能特化，不同网络分别支持语言、形式推理、心理推理和物理世界推理。一个核心未解问题：这种模块化组织究竟是智能系统构建的基本原则，还是生物大脑在进化过程中特有的偶然产物？该研究旨在通过对比LLM这一经由完全不同的优化过程产生的智能系统来回答这一问题。

Method: 使用电路分析（circuit analyses）方法，在N=46个覆盖四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务上对LLM进行神经回路层面的解剖分析，检测任务间是否招募重叠或不同的神经元，并与人类功能性神经网络的划分进行跨物种/跨系统对照。

Result: LLMs发展出一种镜像人脑的模块化架构：人类中由相同神经网路支持的任务在LLM中会招募重叠的神经元群；而分属不同人类网络的任务在LLM中则激活不同的神经元群。功能模块性在两种系统间收敛性地出现。

Conclusion: 模块化组织在生物大脑和人工神经网络中的收敛性涌现表明，它可能是智能系统的一种基本属性，而非特定的进化路径或优化算法必然导致的偶然现象。这对理解智能的本质以及类脑模块化AI架构的设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [141] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对Chutes平台一年生产轨迹数据的全局特征分析和纵向研究，揭示了LLM服务工作负载的演变规律和用户-模型交互结构，并承诺随论文发布完整的一年期轨迹数据以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务工作负载研究在规模和时间范围上有限，通常观测时间短，且对用户与模型在生产环境中的交互方式可见性不足，无法全面捕捉工作负载随时间的演变以及用户-模型交互对生产流量的影响。

Method: 使用Chutes平台一年期生产轨迹，从聚合、时间、模型级和用户级四个视角进行全局特征分析，并进行纵向（longitudinal）研究，涵盖流行模型和长尾模型。

Result: 揭示了聚合视角下通常被隐藏的工作负载演变模式和用户-模型结构，并将发布完整的一年期轨迹数据（非采样或合成生成），为后续生产行为研究提供基准。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模、长时间跨度且覆盖多模型多用户的真实生产轨迹，填补了现有LLM服务工作负载研究的空白，为服务系统的激励和基准测试提供了更真实的数据基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [142] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentao，一个受治理的本地优先运行时系统，用于工具使用型LLM智能体。该系统通过分层架构将模型生成的动作建议与宿主授权的执行分离，使权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹成为显式的运行时抽象，从而构建更可治理、可检查、适合宿主控制本地环境的智能体。代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益成为调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久记忆并与外部协议交互的执行系统。这些能力带来便利的同时也引入了过度授权、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不可控副作用等安全风险。现有系统缺乏对智能体行为的显式治理机制，因此需要一种能够将动作授权、状态管理和执行追溯形式化的运行时方案。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，包括宿主面对面（host-facing surfaces）、宿主契约（host contract）、运行时核心（runtime core）、权限中介工具系统（permission-mediated tool system），以及支持记忆、重放、插件、技能、子智能体和协议集成的子系统。核心思想是将模型生成的动作提案与宿主授权的实际执行相分离，通过权限机制对所有工具调用进行中介和控制。

Result: Agentao不提供形式化的安全保证，但展示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹构建为显式的运行时抽象，从而使得智能体更可治理、可检查和适合宿主控制的本地环境。论文描述了系统的动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行流水线和结构化事件接口，代码已开源。

Conclusion: Agentao证明了"治理超越模型"的设计理念：通过将安全保障从模型自身转移到宿主控制的运行时基础设施，可以有效增强LLM智能体的可治理性和可审计性，为构建安全的工具使用型智能体提供了一条务实路径。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [143] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI评估的主导范式（以超人类自主性能为目标，隐含取代人类的目的）正将AI发展引向错误方向，应转向评估人机协作团队的表现，以促进AI作为人类能力的真正补充，从而带来更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式过度聚焦于超人类自主性能，本质上以替代人类为目标，这可能导致AI开发方向偏离社会最佳利益。作者认为这一范式存在根本性问题，需要重新审视。

Method: 作为立场论文，本文通过论证方式提出观点：主张将AI评估的中心从单机自主性能转向人机协同团队的整体表现，并提出这一范式转移的逻辑链条。

Result: 论文提出了一个方向性论点，即协作式的评估范式转移将催生真正补充人类能力的AI系统，从而比现行评估流程带来更好的社会结果（未提供实证结果，属于观点阐述）。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：AI社区应从根本上转变评估范式，从追求取代人类转向追求增强人机团队效能，这将导向更优的社会发展路径。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [144] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中高置信度错误的一种新解释：稳定误校准（stable miscalibration），即自信的错答在局部扰动下依然保持稳定，而非脆弱的内部推理。作者结合输出层审计分数与内部隐状态灵敏度探针两个诊断工具，在多领域二元事实审计集上发现，审计分数虽能追踪自批判带来的决策损失降低，但内部探针显示自批判提示主要实现局部稳定化而非简单拒答，且高置信度错误未必比高置信度正确回答更具局部敏感性，表明部分高置信度错误是稳定且误校准的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的高置信度错误通常被视为内部推理脆弱性的证据，但这一假设可能过于简化。研究者希望探索另一种可能性：某些高置信度错误实际上是稳定误校准，即模型在局部扰动下仍坚持错误答案，这暗示其错误源于系统性校准缺陷而非简单的不稳定性。

Method: 采用两个互补的诊断工具：(1) 标签感知的输出级审计分数，在强制回答基线（forced-answer baseline）下根据置信度变化与过度自信错误对领域进行排序；(2) 内部灵敏度探针，测量隐状态在扰动下的变动幅度。两者结合应用于多领域二元事实审计数据集，并在三种开放权重模型上评估自批判提示对隐状态灵敏度的影响。

Result: 审计分数能追踪自批判式自我批评减少决策损失的程度，但直接使用带标签基线排序时效果更强。内部层面，自我批判提示在三个开放权重模型的各层一致降低隐状态灵敏度，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化而非单纯输出层拒答模式。然而，审计定义的过度自信错误并不比自信正确的回答具有明显更高的局部敏感性，表明部分高置信度错误是稳定且误校准的。

Conclusion: 高置信度错误不应一概归因于内部推理的脆弱性；稳定的误校准是其中一类重要现象。自我批判提示通过局部稳定化降低决策损失，但这并不等同于校准改善。该工作为区分"脆弱错误"与"稳定误校准"提供了可操作的双重诊断框架，对理解与改进LLM可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [145] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性指标（BCM），用于量化智能体在执行不同任务时行为的一致性。通过分析约9000条轨迹，发现跨任务一致性与任务内可复现性是相互独立的维度，且一致性不能由成功率简化为单一指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估几乎完全依赖结果指标（如成功率），只能反映智能体是否成功，无法衡量其行为的一致性。作者认为行为一致性是一种独立且可量化的属性，需要专门的度量方法来补充结果指标。

Method: BCM通过训练一个模型，从智能体执行轨迹的行为特征中预测任务成功与否；然后为每条轨迹推导特征归因向量；最后计算智能体系统内这些向量之间的平均成对相似度，作为一致性度量。

Result: 在约9000条来自六个语言模型智能体在软件工程任务上的轨迹中，发现：跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是两个可分离的维度，某些系统在单任务重复尝试中表现出一致性（局部可复现）但在不同任务间缺乏稳定策略（全局碎片化）；一致性不能由成功率推导，成功率相近的系统在一致性上可能有显著差异；在控制任务难度的条件下，前沿模型与开源模型之间的一致性差距仍然存在。

Conclusion: BCM作为过程层面的可靠性信号，能够补充现有的结果指标。作者同时指出了该指标有效性的适用条件，并强调先前的同任务可复现性测量无法观察到跨任务与任务内一致性之间分离这一现象。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [146] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种误解的层级化分析模型，将误解视为"分歧产生→分歧放大→分歧检测与修复"的分层过程，从九个互不引用的研究领域中识别出十一种精确的失效模式，并将其定位到通信过程中的八个分析层级，同时通过正式建模和信息论扩展、证据矩阵、编码手册及九个对话案例分析提供了可审计的研究框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通信从实时面对面互动转向AI中介渠道，沟通者被切断了传统修复机制所依赖的资源，而新的误解检测手段尚未跟上。因此，解决误解检测问题成为当务之急。此外，现有研究中缺乏一个既能将机制定位于过程环节、又能按功能进行类型划分的误解分类体系。

Method: 本文整合了九个通常互不引用的研究领域的文献，从中归纳出十一种精确的失效模式；将这些模式定位于通信过程的具体环节而非任意位置；由此推导出八个来源于文献而非现成模型的分析层级；对八个层级进行了正式建模，将信息论和通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建；并提供了源对源证据矩阵（保证可审计性）、编码手册及九个对话案例分析。

Result: 识别出十一种失效模式，其中八种主要产生分歧，两种主要放大已有分歧，一种主导分歧是否被检测与修复。建立了八个源于文献的分析层级，并对它们进行了正式建模，同时提供了完整的证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话案例以供审计与复现。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分类体系是首个既能将误解机制定位于通信过程的具体环节、又能按功能对其进行类型划分的分类框架，填补了这一研究空白，为AI中介通信场景中的误解检测问题提供了系统化的理论基础和分析工具。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [147] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种主动感知框架，用于具身环境中目标消歧。该框架以主动观察为信息获取主干，利用视觉语言模型基于累积的视觉证据和交互信息，动态决策是继续观察、请求澄清还是完成目标选择。真实机器人实验表明该框架能统一物理信息获取与用户意图澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身环境中目标歧义不仅源于用户意图，还可能源于当前观察中缺失与任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、视角受限、文字不可读、目标未观察到）。现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖询问用户来获取额外信息，无法应对需要机器人主动改变观察的情况。

Method: 提出一个主动感知框架：以主动观察作为信息获取的主干，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）基于累积的视觉证据与交互信息进行决策，在继续观察、请求用户澄清、完成目标选择三种行为之间动态切换。主动观察既可以直接恢复缺失的判别性物理证据，也能揭示对象名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需澄清时提升用户交互质量。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证了该框架能够将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一在同一个具身消歧流程中，实现有效的目标消歧。

Conclusion: 主动观察与交互澄清可以有机结合，构成统一的具身目标消歧机制，弥补了传统仅靠询问用户的消歧方法在物理证据缺失情况下的不足。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [148] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个面向设备端长期记忆的基准与框架，基于年尺度的移动体验数据，通过知识驱动的合成管线构建连贯且时序一致的长期轨迹，支持文本与多模态设置，涵盖多跳推理、时序推理、知识更新和隐含偏好推断等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI智能体正从回答孤立问题转向持久化的个人助理，需要长期记忆来积累和利用用户特定经验。然而现有基准无法充分评估真实移动场景下的长期记忆，因为这些场景中的经验是异质的、多模态的、不断演化的且高度个人化的。

Method: MobileMem采用知识驱动的合成管线，从用户-应用会话中构建连贯且时序一致的长期轨迹；提供互补的文本与多模态设置，覆盖多跳推理、时序推理、知识更新和隐含偏好推断等任务类型。

Result: MobileMem使智能体能够记住过去、理解现在并适应未来，将记忆从孤立的信息检索推进到面向持续个人学习的体验智能。

Conclusion: MobileMem通过建模"体验"而非"孤立事实"，弥补了现有长期记忆基准在真实移动场景下的不足，为设备端长期记忆研究提供了更贴近实际生活形态的评估框架。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [149] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大型语言模型的应用中会话交接（session handover）的方法论和理论性质，将交接形式化为任务相对上下文学习（ICL）状态的转移，建立了预测等价性下的充分交接理论，提出了一个三部分记录方案，并给出了高斯线性回归和非参数回归下的内存-误差边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM应用中，当上下文达到模型输入限制、应用重启或由另一代理接手任务时，任务需要在新会话中继续。此时必须决定从先前会话中传递哪些信息。现有方法缺乏对交接问题的系统理论，不清楚哪些信息必须保留、保留量如何依赖于后续任务。

Method: 将交接形式化为任务相对ICL状态的转移，区分"精确恢复早期材料"与"保留目标分布"两种目标。在外生性条件下，用预测等价性刻画最粗的确定性充分交接并给出定长比特需求。提出三部分记录方案：(1) 精确存储决策和约束；(2) 用任务论证统计量处理重复证据；(3) 保留统计量无法保留其效果的原始观测。对高斯线性回归给出精确有限维交接和有限比特扰动界，对非参数回归给出连接内存与平方预测误差的上下界。

Result: 在不依赖下游查询已知的前提下，量化了记忆约束、写入者和续写过程各自的影响。高斯线性回归场景下实现精确有限维交接并导出有限比特扰动界；非参数回归场景下获得内存与平方预测误差之间的上下界。提出的三部分记录方案为设计交接机制提供了具体方法。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM应用中的会话交接提供了第一个系统的理论框架和方法，阐明了交接必须保留什么信息以及内存需求如何依赖于续写任务，为处理上下文限制、应用重启和多代理协作场景中的信息传递问题提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


### [150] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在推理时利用多种信号预测前沿大语言模型更新后产生的样本级回归（即旧模型回答正确但新模型回答错误的情况）。通过统一的新增价值测试，在三个任务族、六个模型更新对上比较单模型信号与跨版本信号，发现信号有效性因任务而异、不存在普适最优信号，并提出了基于选择性回退的proof-of-concept方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM频繁更新，虽然在总体上通常优于前代，但在个别样本上可能出现回归：旧模型回答正确、新模型回答错误。聚合指标掩盖了这种样本级退化，而现有研究缺乏对预测此类回归信号的系统性比较。

Method: 在两项框架下系统比较信号：单模型信号（置信度、logit边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表示漂移），使用统一的新增价值测试来隔离各信号相对于置信度基线的增益。实验覆盖六个基准、三个任务族（多选题问答、数学推理、代码生成）和六个模型更新对。

Result: （1）信号有效性依赖任务：置信度在MCQ和简单数学上最强，而似然/KL信号在较难数学和代码任务上增益最多；（2）不存在跨模型更新普适最优的信号；（3）部分跨版本信号即使在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，且无需标签，从而支撑了将高风险样本回退到旧模型的选择性回退方案。

Conclusion: 实践者可根据任务层面的模式为具体模型更新选择合适的回归预测信号。作者提供了开源代码以支持复现与应用。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [151] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标注基准的持续学习工具（Continual Learning Harnesses）评估框架，基于规模假设，通过教师模型提供稀疏校正来衡量学生模型向教师收敛的程度，并以"教师相对提升"（teacher-relative lift）作为无标注场景下真实工具增益的代理指标，在多种安全任务、模型家族和工具设计中验证了该指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习工具在网络安全领域虽有价值，但其评估依赖标注基准，而运维安全场景中标注标签稀缺、过时且不具代表性，导致实践者无法判断工具是否有帮助或哪一工具更优。传统LLM-as-a-judge因评判能力不优于被评对象而缺乏信号，蒸馏方法在稀疏、零散且带偏的标签下也不可靠。

Method: 提出一个无标注基准的端到端评估框架：更强的教师模型向配备持续学习工具的较小学生模型提供稀疏采样校正，通过衡量学生随时间向教师收敛的程度来评分工具。在安全任务、模型家族和工具设计等多个维度上，验证教师相对提升与留出黄金标准下的真实提升之间的相关性。

Result: 在多种安全任务、模型家族和工具设计下，学生相对于教师的提升与相对于留出黄金标准的提升相关，验证了教师相对提升可作为无标签场景下真实工具增益的代理指标。同时发现，同等级模型之间的LLM-as-a-judge无法提供可用信号。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，当人类提供与教师模型相同的稀疏、高精度校正时，教师规模的模型可以通过相同的持续学习工具得到提升，为无标注场景下的持续学习工具评估提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness.
