We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [219] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI自我改进反馈回路可能引发智能爆炸的数学条件，指出奇点型增长（垂直渐近线）比经济学启发模型预测的更难以实现，并揭示了一类此前被忽视的"超指数但无垂直渐近线"的增长速率，强调生成时间（feedback loop每轮所需时间）是决定智能爆炸行为的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正被用于辅助AI研发，这种反馈回路在特定条件下可能产生智能爆炸。作者希望从数学上探索最具爆炸性的可能性，理解驱动动力学的根本因素，并纠正现有经济学启发模型中对该问题的理解偏差。

Method: 通过数学建模与分析，系统研究AI自我改进反馈循环下的增长动力学。具体包括：对比不同增长模型的数学性质，证明奇异增长（趋向垂直渐近线）的达成条件，识别超指数但非奇异的增长类别，并引入生成时间作为关键参数进行理论分析。

Result: 发现奇异增长比近期经济学启发模型所暗示的更难实现；识别出一类被忽视的增长速率——快于指数但不会导致垂直渐近线；证明生成时间是决定智能爆炸行为的核心参数，且只有当生成时间迅速趋近于零时，奇异增长才可能发生。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸的数学动力学比以往理解更为微妙：垂直渐近线式的奇异增长需要极为苛刻的条件（生成时间必须急速归零），而快于指数但非奇异的增长路径是一个重要且此前被忽视的中间类别。生成时间应被视为分析任何智能爆炸场景的核心变量。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [220] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-Bench，一个基于模拟器的物理适应基准，包含144个从源环境到突变目标环境的配对任务，覆盖六个物理域，用于评估智能体在环境条件变化后的自适应与恢复能力。实验表明该基准远未饱和，最佳的Reflexion+Qwen3-14B仅实现35.9%的成功率，且揭示出机制重设计而非参数推断是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体评估通常在固定的执行条件下优化，无法测试环境条件变化后的恢复能力。为了弥补这一空白，需要一个能够模拟环境突变并评估智能体自适应能力的基准测试。

Method: 构建PACE-Bench基准，包含144个源到目标适应配对，横跨六个物理域。每个配对将源环境链接到具有相同目标和接口的突变目标环境，源代码设计在源中成功但在目标中失败，智能体需利用诊断沙箱反馈在有限的尝试预算内迭代适应。采用四类范式的十种自进化方法进行比较评估。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion+Qwen3-14B仅在全部配对中成功35.9%，GPT-5.5在完整预算下仅解决Statics子集的66.7%。模拟器grounded反思比未经验证的自修订更可靠，记忆机制使智能体锚定早期设计，宽泛的树搜索探索但无法收敛。即使暴露确切的物理变化也不能提高性能天花板。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体在物理环境突变下的适应能力仍存在显著瓶颈，核心挑战在于机制重设计而非参数推断。PACE-Bench为评估和推动该方向的研究提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [221] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，用于自动化生成有信息依据的、多模态技术报告，整合图像、表格、文本与引用，并通过声明自动修订阶段增强内容的事实基础。人工评估和自动评估均表明其在图表信息量、报告有用性及引用准确率等方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动的创新时代，知识增长速度极快，难以跟上；现有生成模型虽然能合成内容，但往往缺乏信息依据（grounding），导致生成内容可能不可靠。需要一种能够生成多模态、且有充分引用支撑的技术报告的自动化框架。

Method: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，支持多模态输出（图像、表格、文本），融合支持性引用生成统一报告。框架特别关注内容的信息依据，引入声明自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段来修正和验证生成内容的准确性。

Result: 人工评估显示：图表信息量在87%的案例中被认为是优于近期基线；Wyvern报告的有用性在63%-100%的实例中被评为优于三种替代方法。自动评估显示：与基线相比，Wyvern在引用召回率上提升高达2.3倍，引用精确率提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern作为一种多智能体多模态技术报告生成框架，通过声明自动修订机制显著提升了生成内容的依据性和可信度，在图表信息量、报告有用性以及引用质量等多项指标上均优于现有基线方法，为AI驱动的知识生成领域提供了一种可靠的技术报告自动化生成方案。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [222] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SheetCompass，一个基于图引导和记忆驱动的智能体框架，用于解决LLM在复杂电子表格推理中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的电子表格包含隐式的跨表关联、细粒度的列依赖和复杂的空间布局，而现有方法将多维结构扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了工作表内边界和跨表语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家在检查电子表格时自然使用的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass显式建模工作表内和工作表间的结构关系，同时在记忆中维护任务相关信息，使智能体能够在复杂工作簿上进行更有效的推理。

Result: 论文摘要中未明确报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过图引导和记忆驱动的机制，SheetCompass能够更好地处理复杂电子表格中的隐式关联和空间布局问题，提升LLM的电子表格推理与自动化能力。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [223] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种移位感知双编码器迁移框架（shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework），用于解决源域与目标域统计特性差异大且目标域数据有限时的PM2.5短时预报问题。该框架结合源域（美国10个监测站）预训练知识与目标域（台湾77个站点）特有表示学习，在冻结源编码器设置下以MSE=21.8960优于现有方法，允许源编码器自适应更新时达到最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异时，仅在本地数据上训练的模型难以捕捉复杂的时间动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移（negative transfer），因此需要设计一种能平衡源域知识与目标域特有信息的迁移框架。

Method: 该方法采用双编码器架构：一个源编码器用美国10个监测站的逐时观测数据进行预训练以捕获通用气象-污染动态知识；另一个目标域专属分支用于学习台湾站点的特有表示。框架按时间顺序划分训练-验证-测试集进行适应和评估，并对比四种主要基线方法，同时进行消融实验和SHAP可解释性分析。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四个基线中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960, MAE=3.1597, R²=0.8725），比TL-v1降低约7.1%的MSE，比TL-v2降低约4.1%。消融实验表明移除台湾专属分支导致性能下降最大；允许源编码器自适应更新时达到全局最优（MSE=21.6575, MAE=3.1383, R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析显示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测值和与污染物传输及扩散相关的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特有信息且允许迁移表示在目标监督下自适应调整时最有效。这一发现验证了"shift-aware"双编码器设计对应对领域偏移的有效性，为跨区域PM2.5短时预报提供了可行的迁移学习范式。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [224] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了测试时世界模型推断（Twin）系统，由前沿编码智能体编写可执行的世界模型来完成持续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）。系统通过模拟和交互自动构建游戏规则与目标，不需要人工为每个任务定制设计。Twin在183个关卡中清除了179个（97.8%），且在88.3%的关卡中比人类更高效，将基础模型得分从7.8%提升至93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个任务手工设计世界模型，成本高且难以泛化。面对持续学习任务（如网格游戏），每个游戏隐藏规则和目标，需要一个能自动从模拟与交互中推断世界模型的通用系统。

Method: Twin系统由前沿编码智能体编写可执行的世界模型，采用强归纳先验对网格游戏建模。系统通过"孪生世界模型"进行重放验证，强制要求程序在复现所有先前观察到的游戏转换后才能执行动作。每次世界模型预测与实际动作结果不匹配都会产生反例，用于修复和完善世界模型。

Result: Twin清除了183个关卡中的179个（97.8%），在179个清除的关卡中有158个（88.3%）比人类更高效。系统在156个关卡（87.2%）中在获得任何奖励前就推断出目标，其余关卡通过搜索自动发现目标。基准测试中，基础模型直接得分为7.8%，现成脚手架提升到61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型提升到93.3%，清除25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 构建可用的世界模型比预想的更简单，而更困难的问题是推断正确的目标。世界模型推断本身可以很好地自动化解决，但目标推断仍是最具挑战性的部分。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [225] [Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509)
*Zhelun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Systems that ask a language model to reach a conclusion from many sources usually concatenate them into one prompt. This conflates two operations with different requirements. Interpreting a source rewards capacity and context. Combining interpretations rewards fixed arithmetic, comparability across instances, and the option to return nothing. Once separated, the design problem becomes the interface between them. We propose a four-field evidence tuple (hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance) and show that fixing it determines both halves. The separation also reveals a failure mode in how such systems combine, which we call count-scale drift. Thresholding a sum of unnormalized weights is exactly posterior thresholding, but at an operating point that slides with the number of sources consulted. The slide grows with reader reliability. When source reliabilities differ, the vote rule and the posterior order instances differently, and no threshold reconciles them. Pooling calibrated log-likelihood ratios addresses both problems. The fix is arithmetic rather than architectural, and applies to a class of rules beyond language models: score-summing triage engines, diagnostic panels scored by counting positives, and additive multi-signal detectors. We then instantiate the principle twice on one longitudinal corpus, once after outcomes resolve and once before. The same partition helps in both, at different granularities: over reading in the first, over learning capacity in the second. There, a small sequence encoder on an easy auxiliary objective plus a tree ensemble carrying the censored survival loss reaches 0.921 AUPRC against 0.805 for a hand-crafted baseline. We separate what transfers from what must be re-estimated per domain, and state five predictions that would falsify the framework, three negative results, and which comparisons remain confounded.

</details>


### [226] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段实验（N=809）在三个部署情境中系统研究了道德AI偏好引导流水线的三个关键环节——特征界定、选民抽样和问题框架——并证明每一个环节都会显著影响AI道德偏好引导产出的结果。研究指出，道德偏好引导不能仅靠聚合投票实现公平透明的AI，每一阶段的决策都应被审计和公开披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地承担社会层面的道德决策，研究者采用"道德偏好引导"方法：通过向参与者收集假设困境的投票来训练AI策略。然而，在投票发生之前，开发者需要做出三个关键选择（特征界定、选民抽样、问题框架），这些选择往往不透明、未记录，且被视为技术细节而非规范性决策。作者旨在揭示这些被忽视的选择如何实质性地影响道德偏好引导的输出结果。

Method: 研究采用两阶段实验设计（N=809），横跨三个部署情境：AI肾脏分配、AI代理模拟缺勤员工、生成式AI再现逝者。在每个情境中，作者系统检验了道德AI偏好引导流水线的三个阶段：首先考察道德相关特征在不同情境间的转移性；其次分析政治意识形态对特征偏好的影响；最后检验问题措辞对意识形态群体间分歧的影响以及道德基础与判断关联的变化。

Result: 研究发现：第一，道德相关特征在不同部署情境间存在显著转移，不能假设特征模式可跨域迁移；第二，约三分之一的特征上，参与者的偏好因政治意识形态而异，且部分差异方向相反，因此选民池的意识形态构成会影响聚合偏好；第三，问题措辞可窄化或扩大意识形态分歧达整整一个量表点，同时框架条件也改变了道德基础与参与者判断之间的关联方式。

Conclusion: 三项关键选择都会系统性塑造道德偏好引导的产出结果，这意味着仅依靠投票聚合无法实现公平或透明的AI。结论建议至少应对道德AI偏好引导流水线的每个阶段进行审计和公开披露，将其视为需要规范性审查的环节，而非单纯的技术细节。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629)
*Oscar L. Cruz-Gonzalez,Valérie Deplano,Badih Ghattas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出M3PI-DeepONet，一种改进的多输入多输出物理信息深度算子网络，用于预测腹主动脉瘤（AAA）中的非定常三维血流，仅需0.3%的标注内部数据和物理约束，即可实现速度误差<4%、压力误差约5%，推理速度比CFD快约36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上对AAA进行个性化血流动力学评估（壁面剪切应力、涡结构、压力分布）至关重要，但传统CFD计算开销大。虽然PI-DeepONet在补充CFD方面有前景，但现有架构在处理复杂三维流动时仍面临挑战，需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出M3PI-DeepONet架构，核心创新包括：(1) 聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）策略，将多个输入分支的潜在表示在注入trunk之前融合，使坐标基适应多个物理约束；(2) 首次将逐层门控机制（layer-wise gating）与多分支算子网络拓扑结合，形成输入自适应的trunk基；(3) 集成三维Navier-Stokes方程作为物理规律，基于物理信息残差、初始和边界条件训练，仅使用0.3%的标注内部数据和选定的分支条件信号。

Result: M3PI-DeepONet同时预测非定常三维血流速度场和压力场，平均相对L2速度误差低于4%，压力误差约5%；与参考CFD模拟相比，在分支输入可用后实现约36倍的保守循环推理加速。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了深度学习在心血管疾病建模中的应用，为实时、无创的临床诊断迈出了重要一步，验证了结合多分支算子网络与物理约束在复杂三维血流预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Clinically actionable, patient-specific hemodynamic assessment, specifically wall shear stress, vortex structure and pressure distributions, is critical for determining risky or unfavorable evolution in Abdominal Aortic Aneurysms (AAA). While Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) show promising results in complementing established 5 tools such as Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), a persistent architectural challenge remains for complex 3D flows. In this direction, we propose a Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets (M3PI-DeepONet) designed for predicting unsteady flows in an idealized AAA geometry. Central to our model is the Aggregated Injection strategy, where latent representations from multiple input branches are fused prior to trunk injection, allowing the coordinate basis to adapt to multiple physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first architecture to combine the layer-wise gating mechanism with a multi-branch operator-network topology, yielding an input-adaptive trunk basis. Additionally, we integrate the 3D Navier-Stokes equations as governing physical laws, so the model is trained based on physics-informed residuals, initial and boundary conditions, and only 0.3% of the labeled internal data together with the selected branch-conditioning signals. The M3PI-DeepONet simultaneously predicts unsteady 3D flow velocity and pressure fields with an average relative L2 velocity error below 4% and pressure error around 5% while achieving a conservative retained-cycle inference speedup of approximately 36x compared to reference CFD simulations once the branch inputs used for conditioning are available. This work advances the application of deep learning in cardiovascular disease modeling, marking step toward real-time, non-invasive clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [228] [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Christopher P. Muller,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了极大似然估计（MLE）在训练高斯过程（GP）模型用于概率回归和分类任务时的脆弱性，提出了基于理论支撑的替代度量指标和实用解决方案，并在贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中验证了其有效性，表明所训练的GP在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本上甚至可超越tabular foundation models。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE是训练机器学习模型最流行的机制之一，但其假设若不满足则会导致模型泛化能力差。GP在工程设计中被广泛使用，常被错误地认为对过拟合非常鲁棒，实际上同样受MLE脆弱性的影响。因此需要对MLE在GP训练中的脆弱性进行根本性的评估并提供实用解决方案。

Method: 作者将理论上有依据的度量指标与MLE进行对比，系统评估MLE在训练GP进行概率回归或分类任务时的脆弱性，并提出实用的替代训练方案，通过广泛的实验在贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中验证所提方法的有效性。

Result: 大量研究表明所提出的解决方案在下游设计任务（如贝叶斯优化）中表现出有效性。训练得到的GP在预测精度、不确定性量化以及推理成本方面甚至优于tabular foundation models。

Conclusion: 本文为工程实践者提供了构建准确且鲁棒GP的蓝图，并公开了代码实现，有助于推动GP在工程设计中的可靠应用。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become an indispensable part of modern engineering design workflows. A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting. In this paper, we fundamentally evaluate the brittleness of MLE in the context of training GPs for probabilistic regression or classification tasks. We compare theoretically grounded metrics against MLE and propose practical solutions. Our extensive studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our solutions in downstream design tasks such as Bayesian optimization and provide a blueprint for practitioners to build accurate and robust GPs that can even outperform tabular foundation models in terms of prediction accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and inference cost. Our contributions are publicly available via GitHub at https://github.com/Bostanabad-Research-Group/GP-vs-TabPFN-vs-GPyTorch.

</details>


### [229] [Extending Occam's inversion with lasso fusion, overcomplete dictionaries, and isotropic total variation regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14225)
*Anandaroop Ray*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在Occam反演框架下使用l1正则化（总变差/融合套索）进行地球物理反演的方法，统一了合成（analysis）与分析（synthesis）两种求解形式，并比较了坐标下降、迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）和分裂Bregman三种求解器在1D和2D问题（包括非线性电磁探测及野外数据）上的性能，建议1D问题用坐标下降、2D问题用IRLS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Occam反演使用l2模型粗糙度惩罚，得到平滑模型但抑制了地质边界等尖锐特征。虽然l1正则化可以产生视觉上锐利的模型，但地球物理学中对l1多维正则化（总变差）的两种不同形式（边缘方向不变与不不变）区分不足，且与统计学、影像学领域的最新进展脱节。作者希望将l1正则化反演统一到地球物理学家熟悉的Occam框架下，消除误解并推广应用。

Method: 作者首先在1D情形下证明，对于l2数据范数和l1模型正则化，fused lasso通过合成（synthesis）路径和分析（analysis）路径均导出相同的1D问题。随后将框架推广到多维，并纳入小波字典等算子，推导了坐标下降、迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）和分裂Bregman三种常见l1求解器的数学原理。最后通过1D回归、2D去模糊（线性问题）和1D航空瞬变电磁（非线性问题及野外数据）进行系统验证与比较。

Result: 三种求解器均能有效求解l1正则化Occam反演问题。对于1D问题，坐标下降计算效率最高；对于2D问题，IRLS相比分裂Bregman所需的最小二乘求解次数可减少一个数量级，性能更优。野外电磁数据示例验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过将l1正则化反演的各种形式统一呈现为地球物理学家所熟悉的Occam反演范式，本文促进l1正则化（产生锐利模型）在地球物理反演中的采用，同时澄清了l1多维正则化中关于边缘方向不变性的关键区别，为实际应用提供了求解器选型指南。

Abstract: Occam's inversion is a robust algorithm to perform nonlinear geophysical inversion. It provides the smoothest model within observation noise, thereby discouraging geological overinterpretation. While Occam originally penalised l2 model roughness, l1 can be used to provide models that are visually sharp. However, l1 regularised geophysical inversion has diverged from the larger body of statistics and imaging literature. For example, l1 regularisation with a difference operator (i.e., total variation) in multiple dimensions has two distinct forms, only one of which is invariant to edge orientation -- a distinction often overlooked in geophysics. In one dimension this reduces to the fused lasso problem in statistics. Within the Occam framework, for l2 data norm and l1 model regularisation, we show that lasso fusion through either synthesis or analysis leads to the same 1D problem. We extend the framework to multiple dimensions and to operators such as wavelet transforms in a dictionary, unravelling the mathematics behind three commonly used solvers for l1 regularised problems. These are coordinate descent, iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS), and split Bregman. We use them to solve a sequence of problems that are linear (1D regression, 2D deblurring) and nonlinear (1D airborne transient electromagnetics) including a field data example. We recommend coordinate descent for 1D problems, and IRLS over split Bregman for 2D problems, with IRLS requiring up to an order of magnitude fewer least squares solves. We hope this work will further encourage geophysicists to adopt l1 regularised inversion, by presenting its various forms as a familiar Occam's inversion.

</details>


### [230] [Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401)
*Xiaohong Chen,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Jerry Zhijian Yang,Chen Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线强化学习新框架，将最优贝尔曼算子估计与值函数学习解耦。通过条件扩散模型估计奖励函数和转移核，从而得到数据驱动的贝尔曼算子近似，再通过最小化经验贝尔曼残差来学习Q*的深度估计器。理论上建立了算子学习的非渐近收敛率，并在不依赖完备性假设的条件下，得到了关于Q*的L2收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，估计最优动作值函数Q*可归结为基于离线观测求解最优贝尔曼方程。核心难点在于奖励函数和转移核未知，导致最优贝尔曼算子无法直接从数据中观测。现有深度RL理论通常依赖完备性假设（completeness assumptions），限制了理论适用性，亟需一种无需该假设的新框架。

Method: 提出解耦框架：首先利用条件扩散模型估计奖励律（reward law）和转移核（transition kernel），从而构造数据驱动的最优贝尔曼算子近似；随后将这些估计器代入贝尔曼方程，在神经网络函数类上最小化经验贝尔曼残差以获得Q*的深度估计器。

Result: 理论上：(1)通过对条件扩散估计的全端到端分析（在全变差距离下），建立了学习最优贝尔曼算子的尖锐非渐近收敛率；(2)建立了超额贝尔曼残差风险的oracle值阶收敛率O(n^{-2β/(dx+da+2β)})；(3)在可集中性条件下，得到Q*深度估计器的L2收敛率O(n^{-β/(dx+da+2β)})。数值实验验证了方法的有效性和强经验性能。

Conclusion: 该框架成功绕过了传统深度RL理论中常见的完备性假设，通过解耦算子估计与值函数学习，实现了严格的理论保证。理论分析不依赖完备性假设，且实验结果验证了方法在实际中的有效性。

Abstract: In offline RL, estimating the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ can be formulated as solving the optimal Bellman equation based solely on offline observations. A fundamental challenge is that the reward function and transition kernel are unknown, so the optimal Bellman operator is not directly observable from data. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that decouples operator estimation from value function learning. In this approach, we first formulate conditional diffusion models to estimate the reward law and transition kernel, which induces a data-driven approximation of the optimal Bellman operator. We then plug these estimators into the Bellman equation and obtain a deep estimator of $Q^*$ by minimizing the empirical Bellman residual over a neural network function class. Theoretically, we first establish sharp nonasymptotic convergence rates for learning the optimal Bellman operator through an end-to-end analysis of conditional diffusion estimation in total variation distance. We then establish the oracle value-stage rate $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\frac{2β}{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the excess Bellman residual risk. Finally, under a concentrability condition, we translate this residual bound into an $L^2$ convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\fracβ{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the resulting deep estimator of $Q^*$, where $d_x$ and $d_a$ denote the dimensions of the state and action spaces, respectively, and $β$ denotes the Hölder smoothness index of $Q^*$. Importantly, our theoretical analysis does not rely on completeness assumptions commonly used in deep RL theory. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method and its strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [231] [Online Inference in Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408)
*Yang Peng,Liangyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定策略下收益分布泛函的在线统计推断问题。针对Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器，证明了其在Cramér空间中的根T误差弱收敛于中心化高斯随机元，并证明了条件于观测轨迹的自举（bootstrap）估计收敛于同一高斯极限。这一理论为平滑统计泛函（包括方差、CVaR、期望短缺和expectiles）的推断提供了依据；对于非平滑统计泛函，则发展了局部渐近理论及其bootstrap类比，支持包括收益分位数在内的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中基于回报分布（return distribution）的统计推断是重要且具有挑战性的问题。现有分布时域差分（distributional TD）学习主要关注估计本身的收敛性，缺乏对统计泛函进行推断（如置信区间构建）的理论保证。本文旨在填补这一空白，为固定策略下的回报分布泛函提供完整且可操作的推断理论。

Method: 本文采用非参数分布时域差分（distributional temporal-difference learning）方法从单一马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布。具体方法包括：（1）对Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器，在Cramér空间中证明根T误差的弱收敛性以及bootstrap一致性；（2）对平滑泛函直接利用高斯极限进行推断；（3）对非平滑泛函，在T^{-1/2}邻域内建立局部渐近理论及其bootstrap类比，通过CDF方程刻画特征进行推断。

Result: 证明了Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器的根T误差弱收敛到Cramér空间中的中心化高斯随机元；条件于观测轨迹的bootstrap差异同样弱收敛到相同高斯极限。平滑统计泛函（方差、CVaR、期望短缺、expectiles）的bootstrap推断得以证明成立；非平滑统计泛函（如收益分位数）通过局部渐近理论及bootstrap类比获得推断保证。

Conclusion: 本文为固定策略下的回报分布提供了统一的、基于bootstrap的在线统计推断框架，涵盖平滑与非平滑泛函，为强化学习中的不确定性量化提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We study online statistical inference for functionals of the return distribution under a fixed policy. The return distribution is estimated by nonparametric distributional temporal-difference learning from a single Markov trajectory. For the Polyak--Ruppert averaged estimator, we prove that its root-$T$ error converges weakly to a centered Gaussian random element in Cramér space. We also prove that, conditionally on the observed trajectory, the root-$T$ difference between the bootstrap and original averages converges weakly to the same Gaussian limit. These results justify bootstrap inference for smooth statistical functionals, including variance, CVaR, expected shortfall, and expectiles. For nonsmooth statistical functionals, we develop a local asymptotic theory for the estimated return CDF over $T^{-1/2}$-neighborhoods of finitely many thresholds, together with its bootstrap analogue. This theory allows us to conduct inference for nonsmooth statistical functionals characterized by CDF equations, including return quantiles.

</details>


### [232] [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937)
*Yicheng Kang,Yuling Jiao,Xin Geng,Mahesh Nagarajan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出MODERN深度学习框架，用于智能制造中的质量监控和故障隔离，基于inception残差神经网络构建控制图来监控产品缺陷概率，并通过迁移学习进行缺陷区域估计，同时建立了最优收敛速率的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造系统装有许多传感器和成像设备，具备即时通信和智能管理能力，但如何将这些能力有效整合到工业质量控制实践中仍是一个挑战，需要既能监控产品质量又能隔离故障区域的深度学习框架。

Method: 采用inception残差神经网络架构构建监控缺陷概率的控制图；通过迁移学习设计故障区域估计器，识别缺陷区域；针对训练数据不足的情况，提出仅需小样本的迁移监控技术和假设检验方法，用于定量评估方法的适用性；在理论上建立了缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛速率。

Result: 通过模拟实验和真实数据验证，MODERN方法在性能上优于现有最先进的方法；理论结果揭示了一个看似反直觉的管理启示：制造商未必总应不惜成本升级监控设备。

Conclusion: MODERN为智能制造质量监控与故障隔离提供了理论保障与实证支撑的深度学习框架，并提示在设备升级决策中需考虑成本效益而非一味追求技术升级。

Abstract: Smart manufacturing processes are often installed with a large number of sensors, imaging devices and computers, which not only enable instant communication across various modules of a production system but also aid in intelligent manufacturing management. In this paper, we introduce MODERN, a deep learning framework for quality monitoring and fault isolation, which integrates these enhanced capabilities into the practice of industrial quality control. Using the architecture of an inception residual neural network, we develop a control chart that monitors the likelihood of a product containing defects. We also propose a faulty region estimator that identifies the defective area using transfer learning. To extend our framework to cases where there are not sufficient training data, we suggest a transfer monitoring technique that requires only a small sample size and a hypothesis testing approach for quantitatively assessing the applicability of our method. Theoretically, we establish the minimax optimal convergence rate for both our defect likelihood estimation and fault diagnosis. Our results lead to a seemingly counter-intuitive managerial implication - it may not always be in a manufacturer's best interests to keep upgrading its monitoring equipment regardless of the cost. Empirically, we demonstrate the superior performance of our method in comparison with a state-of-the-art approach using both simulated experiments and real data.
